

===== OUTPUT_generate_use_case OUTPUT =====
Here’s the structured Use Case for the advanced e-shop backend system:

---

### **Use Case: Manage Users in E-Shop Backend**
**Primary Actor:** Administrator
**Goal:** Create or delete users in the system while maintaining consistency of carts, reservations, and stock.

**System Responsibilities:**
- Maintain a registry of users with unique IDs.
- Automatically create an empty cart for each new user.
- Prevent duplicate user creation.
- Handle user deletion by removing their cart, canceling reservations, and updating stock.
- Preserve existing orders of deleted users.

**Preconditions:**
- The system is operational and accessible.

**Postconditions:**
- A new user is created with an empty cart, or an existing user is deleted with their cart and reservations removed.
- Stock is updated if products from canceled reservations still exist.

**Main Success Scenario (Create User):**
1. Administrator requests to create a user with a unique ID.
2. System validates the ID is not already in use.
3. System creates the user and assigns an empty cart.
4. System confirms successful creation.

**Extensions:**
2a. User ID already exists → System rejects the request and notifies the administrator.
1-4a. Invalid input (e.g., empty ID) → System ignores the request.

**Main Success Scenario (Delete User):**
1. Administrator requests to delete a user by ID.
2. System validates the user exists.
3. System removes the user’s cart and cancels all their reservations.
4. For each canceled reservation:
   - If the product exists in stock, the reserved quantity is returned to available stock.
   - If the product no longer exists, the reservation is removed without stock updates.
5. System confirms successful deletion.

**Extensions:**
2a. User does not exist → System ignores the request.
3-5a. System fails to process reservations → No changes are applied (atomicity).

---

### **Use Case: Manage Products in E-Shop Backend**
**Primary Actor:** Administrator
**Goal:** Add or delete products while maintaining stock consistency and preserving existing reservations/orders.

**System Responsibilities:**
- Maintain a product catalog with names, prices, and stock.
- Allow zero stock for products.
- Update existing products if a duplicate name is provided (increase stock, update price).
- Delete products only from available stock, leaving reservations/orders intact.

**Preconditions:**
- The system is operational.

**Postconditions:**
- A new product is added or an existing product is updated/deleted.
- Available stock reflects changes without affecting reservations/orders.

**Main Success Scenario (Add/Update Product):**
1. Administrator requests to add a product with name, price, and stock.
2. System checks if the product name already exists.
3. If the product exists:
   - System increases its stock by the provided quantity.
   - System updates its price to the new value.
4. If the product does not exist:
   - System creates a new product entry.
5. System confirms the operation.

**Extensions:**
1a. Invalid input (e.g., negative price/stock) → System ignores the request.
2-4a. System fails to update stock → No changes are applied.

**Main Success Scenario (Delete Product):**
1. Administrator requests to delete a product by name.
2. System validates the product exists in available stock.
3. System removes the product from available stock.
4. System confirms deletion.

**Extensions:**
2a. Product does not exist → System ignores the request.
3a. Product has active reservations/orders → System retains them unchanged.

---

### **Use Case: Modify User Cart**
**Primary Actor:** User
**Goal:** Add or remove products from their cart, creating/updating reservations and adjusting stock.

**System Responsibilities:**
- Validate product availability before adding to cart.
- Block reserved quantities from other users.
- Handle cart modifications atomically.
- Return stock if products are removed from the cart.

**Preconditions:**
- The user exists and has an active cart.

**Postconditions:**
- The cart reflects the requested changes.
- Reservations and stock are updated accordingly.

**Main Success Scenario (Add Product to Cart):**
1. User requests to add a product with a specified quantity to their cart.
2. System validates the product exists and the quantity is available in stock.
3. System deducts the quantity from available stock and creates/updates a reservation.
4. System confirms the addition.

**Extensions:**
2a. Product does not exist or quantity is unavailable → System ignores the request.
2b. Requested quantity is zero → System ignores the request.
3a. Product already in cart → System increases the reserved quantity.

**Main Success Scenario (Remove Product from Cart):**
1. User requests to remove a product with a specified quantity from their cart.
2. System validates the product exists in the cart and the quantity does not exceed the reserved amount.
3. System returns the quantity to available stock (if the product still exists) and updates the reservation.
4. System confirms the removal.

**Extensions:**
2a. Product not in cart or quantity exceeds reservation → System ignores the request.
3a. Product no longer exists in stock → System removes the reservation without stock updates.

---

### **Use Case: Perform Checkout**
**Primary Actor:** User
**Goal:** Convert a cart into an order with atomic success/failure handling.

**System Responsibilities:**
- Validate cart contents and product existence.
- Create an order with a unique sequential ID and price snapshot.
- Clear the cart and reservations on success.
- Ensure atomicity (no partial changes on failure).

**Preconditions:**
- The user has a non-empty cart.

**Postconditions:**
- A new order is created with status "pending," or the checkout fails with no changes.

**Main Success Scenario:**
1. User requests to checkout their cart.
2. System validates all reserved products still exist in stock.
3. System calculates the total price using current product prices.
4. System generates a unique order ID (e.g., "SK00001").
5. System creates an order with status "pending" and stores the price snapshot.
6. System clears the cart and reservations.
7. System confirms the order.

**Extensions:**
2a. Any reserved product no longer exists → System cancels checkout and notifies the user.
4a. Order ID generation fails → System cancels checkout.
6a. System fails to clear cart/reservations → No order is created (atomic rollback).

---

### **Use Case: Update Order Status**
**Primary Actor:** Administrator
**Goal:** Transition an order’s status while enforcing valid sequences and handling cancellations.

**System Responsibilities:**
- Enforce valid status transitions (e.g., pending → processing → completed).
- Allow cancellation from any state.
- Restore stock for canceled orders (recreate products if necessary).

**Preconditions:**
- The order exists.

**Postconditions:**
- The order’s status is updated, or the request is ignored.

**Main Success Scenario:**
1. Administrator requests to update an order’s status.
2. System validates the transition is allowed (e.g., pending → processing).
3. System updates the status.
4. System confirms the update.

**Extensions:**
2a. Invalid transition (e.g., delivered → processing) → System ignores the request.
3a. Transition to "cancelled":
   - For each product in the order:
     - If the product exists, increase its stock by the ordered quantity.
     - If the product no longer exists, recreate it with the order’s stored price.
4a. Order is already "cancelled" → System ignores further status changes.

---

### **Use Case: Access System State**
**Primary Actor:** Administrator/User
**Goal:** Retrieve current system state (users, products, stock, carts, orders).

**System Responsibilities:**
- Provide read-only access to:
  - Current users and their carts.
  - Products (name, price, available stock).
  - Reservations and orders with statuses.

**Preconditions:**
- The system is operational.

**Postconditions:**
- The requested state is returned without modifications.

**Main Success Scenario:**
1. Actor requests system state (e.g., list of users).
2. System retrieves and returns the data.

**Extensions:**
1a. Invalid request (e.g., non-existent entity) → System returns an empty or error response.

### **Use Case: Manage Users in E-Shop Backend**
**Primary Actor:** Administrator
**Goal:** Create or delete users in the system while maintaining consistency of carts, reservations, and stock.

**System Responsibilities:**
- Maintain a registry of users with unique IDs.
- Automatically create an empty cart for each new user.
- Prevent duplicate user creation.
- Handle user deletion by removing their cart, canceling reservations, and updating stock.
- Preserve existing orders of deleted users.

**Preconditions:**
- The system is operational and accessible.

**Postconditions:**
- A new user is created with an empty cart, or an existing user is deleted with their cart and reservations removed.
- Stock is updated if products from canceled reservations still exist.
- **Existing orders of deleted users remain unaffected.**

**Main Success Scenario (Create User):**
1. Administrator requests to create a user with a unique ID.
2. System validates the ID is not already in use.
3. System creates the user and assigns an empty cart.
4. System confirms successful creation.

- Validate cart contents and product existence.
- Create an order with a unique sequential ID and price snapshot.

- The user has a non-empty cart.

**Postconditions:**
- A new order is created with status "pending," or the checkout fails with no changes.
- **The order ID is globally unique and strictly sequential across the entire system.**

**Main Success Scenario:**
1. User requests to checkout their cart.
2. System validates all reserved products still exist in stock.
3. System calculates the total price using current product prices.
4. System generates a unique order ID (e.g., "SK00001").
5. System creates an order with status "pending" and stores the price snapshot.

### **Use Case: Update Order Status**
**Primary Actor:** Administrator
**Goal:** Transition an order’s status while enforcing valid sequences and handling cancellations.

**System Responsibilities:**
- Enforce valid status transitions (e.g., pending → processing → completed).
- Allow cancellation from any state.
- Restore stock for canceled orders (recreate products if necessary).
- **Ignore further status changes if the order is in a terminal state (e.g., "cancelled").**

**Preconditions:**
- The order exists.

**Postconditions:**
- The order’s status is updated, or the request is ignored.
- **If the order is cancelled, it becomes a terminal state, and further status changes are ignored.**

**Main Success Scenario:**
1. Administrator requests to update an order’s status.
2. System validates the transition is allowed (e.g., pending → processing).
3. System updates the status.
4. **If the status is set to "cancelled":**
   - For each product in the order:
     - If the product exists, increase its stock by the ordered quantity.
     - If the product no longer exists, recreate it with the order’s stored price.
5. System confirms the update.

**Extensions:**
2a. Invalid transition (e.g., delivered → processing) → System ignores the request.
3a. Transition to "cancelled":
   - For each product in the order:
     - If the product exists, increase its stock by the ordered quantity.
     - If the product no longer exists, recreate it with the order’s stored price.
4a. Order is already "cancelled" → System ignores further status changes.

---

### **Use Case: Access System State**
**Primary Actor:** Administrator/User
**Goal:** Retrieve current system state (users, products, stock, carts, orders).

**System Responsibilities:**
- Provide read-only access to:
  - Current users and their carts.
  - Products (name, price, available stock).
  - Reservations and orders with statuses.

**Preconditions:**
- The system is operational.

**Postconditions:**
- The requested state is returned without modifications.

**Main Success Scenario:**
1. Actor requests system state (e.g., list of users).
2. System retrieves and returns the data, including reservations and orders with statuses.

**Extensions:**
1a. Invalid request (e.g., non-existent entity) → System returns an empty or error response.

### **Use Case: Manage Users in E-Shop Backend**
**Primary Actor:** Administrator
**Goal:** Create or delete users in the system while maintaining consistency of carts, reservations, and stock.

**System Responsibilities:**
- Maintain a registry of users with unique IDs.
- Automatically create an empty cart for each new user.
- Prevent duplicate user creation.
- Handle user deletion by removing their cart, canceling reservations, and updating stock.
- Preserve existing orders of deleted users.

**Preconditions:**
- The system is operational and accessible.

**Postconditions:**
- A new user is created with an empty cart, or an existing user is deleted with their cart and reservations removed.
- Stock is updated if products from canceled reservations still exist.
- **Existing orders of the deleted user remain unaffected in the system.**

**Main Success Scenario (Create User):**
1. Administrator requests to create a user with a unique ID.
2. System validates the ID is not already in use.
3. System creates the user and assigns an empty cart.
4. System confirms successful creation.

**Extensions:**
2a. User ID already exists → System rejects the request and notifies the administrator.
1-4a. Invalid input (e.g., empty ID) → System ignores the request.

**Main Success Scenario (Delete User):**
1. Administrator requests to delete a user by ID.
2. System validates the user exists.
3. System removes the user’s cart and cancels all their reservations.

   - If the product exists in stock, the reserved quantity is returned to available stock.
   - If the product no longer exists, the reservation is removed without stock updates.

**Extensions:**
2a. User does not exist → System ignores the request.
3-5a. System fails to process reservations → No changes are applied (atomicity).

**System Responsibilities:**
- Maintain a product catalog with names, prices, and stock.
- Allow zero stock for products.
- Update existing products if a duplicate name is provided (increase stock, update price).
- Delete products only from available stock, leaving reservations/orders intact.

**Preconditions:**

2a. Product does not exist → System ignores the request.

### **Use Case: Modify User Cart**
**Primary Actor:** User
**Goal:** Add or remove products from their cart, creating/updating reservations and adjusting stock.

**System Responsibilities:**
- Validate product availability before adding to cart.
- Block reserved quantities from other users.
- Handle cart modifications atomically.
- Return stock if products are removed from the cart.

**Preconditions:**
- The user exists and has an active cart.

**Postconditions:**
- The cart reflects the requested changes.
- Reservations and stock are updated accordingly.
- **Reserved quantities are not available to other users.**

**Main Success Scenario (Add Product to Cart):**
1. User requests to add a product with a specified quantity to their cart.
2. System validates the product exists and the quantity is available in stock.
3. System deducts the quantity from available stock and creates/updates a reservation.
4. System confirms the addition.

**Extensions:**
2a. Product does not exist or quantity is unavailable → System ignores the request.
2b. Requested quantity is zero → System ignores the request.
3a. Product already in cart → System **increases the reserved quantity by the new quantity**.

**Main Success Scenario (Remove Product from Cart):**
1. User requests to remove a product with a specified quantity from their cart.
2. System validates the product exists in the cart and the quantity does not exceed the reserved amount.
3. System returns the quantity to available stock (if the product still exists) and updates the reservation.
4. System confirms the removal.

**Extensions:**
2a. Product not in cart or quantity exceeds reservation → System ignores the request.
3a. Product no longer exists in stock → System removes the reservation without stock updates.

### **Use Case: Perform Checkout**
**Primary Actor:** User
**Goal:** Convert a cart into an order with atomic success/failure handling.

**System Responsibilities:**
- Validate cart contents and product existence.
- Create an order with a unique sequential ID and price snapshot.
- Clear the cart and reservations on success.
- Ensure atomicity (no partial changes on failure).

**Preconditions:**
- The user has a non-empty cart.

**Postconditions:**
- A new order is created with status "pending," or the checkout fails with no changes.
- **The order ID is globally unique and strictly sequential across the entire system.**

**Main Success Scenario:**
1. User requests to checkout their cart.
2. System validates all reserved products still exist in stock.
3. System calculates the total price using current product prices.
4. System **generates a globally unique and strictly sequential order ID (e.g., "SK00001")**.
5. System creates an order with status "pending" and stores the price snapshot.
6. System clears the cart and reservations.
7. System confirms the order.

**Extensions:**
2a. Any reserved product no longer exists → System cancels checkout and notifies the user.
4a. Order ID generation fails → System cancels checkout.
6a. System fails to clear cart/reservations → No order is created (atomic rollback).

---

### **Use Case: Update Order Status**
**Primary Actor:** Administrator
**Goal:** Transition an order’s status while enforcing valid sequences and handling cancellations.

**System Responsibilities:**
- Enforce valid status transitions (e.g., pending → processing → completed).
- Allow cancellation from any state.
- Restore stock for canceled orders (recreate products if necessary).
- **Ignore further status changes if the order is in a terminal state (e.g., "cancelled").**

**Preconditions:**
- The order exists.

**Postconditions:**
- The order’s status is updated, or the request is ignored.
- **If the order is cancelled, it becomes a terminal state, and further status changes are ignored.**

**Main Success Scenario:**
1. Administrator requests to update an order’s status.
2. System validates the transition is allowed (e.g., pending → processing).
3. System updates the status.
4. **If the status is set to "cancelled":**
   - For each product in the order:
     - If the product exists, increase its stock by the ordered quantity.
     - If the product no longer exists, **recreate it with the price stored in the order**.
5. **If the order is already in a terminal state (e.g., "cancelled"), the system ignores the request.**
6. System confirms the update.

**Extensions:**
2a. Invalid transition (e.g., delivered → processing) → System ignores the request.
3a. Transition to "cancelled":
   - For each product in the order:
     - If the product exists, increase its stock by the ordered quantity.
     - If the product no longer exists, recreate it with the price stored in the order.
4a. Order is already "cancelled" → System ignores further status changes.

---

### **Use Case: Access System State**
**Primary Actor:** Administrator/User
**Goal:** Retrieve current system state (users, products, stock, carts, orders).

**System Responsibilities:**
- Provide read-only access to:
  - Current users and their carts.
  - Products (name, price, available stock).
  - Reservations and orders with statuses.

**Preconditions:**
- The system is operational.

**Postconditions:**
- The requested state is returned without modifications.

**Main Success Scenario:**
1. Actor requests system state (e.g., list of users).
2. System **provides read-only access to the data, including reservations and orders with their statuses**.

**Extensions:**
1a. Invalid request (e.g., non-existent entity) → System returns an empty or error response.

However, the User Task specifies that adding a product already in the cart should 'only modify and increase the number of given products in the reservation.' The Use Case does not explicitly validate that the new quantity is available in stock before increasing the reservation, which could lead to inconsistent stock handling.",
    "In the 'Perform Checkout' Use Case, the Main Success Scenario does not explicitly state that the checkout must fail if any reserved product no longer exists in the stock system, as required by the User Task. While the extension 2a mentions this, it should be part of the main scenario for clarity.",
    "In the 'Update Order Status' Use Case, the Main Success Scenario does not explicitly state that transitioning to 'cancelled' must recreate products that no longer exist in the stock system with the price stored in the order, as required by the User Task. This is only mentioned in the extensions, which could lead to oversight.",
    "In the 'Update Order Status' Use Case, the postcondition states: 'If the order is cancelled, it becomes a terminal state, and further status changes are ignored.' However, the User Task specifies that 'cancelled' is a terminal state, and the Use Case does not explicitly state that transitions from 'delivered' to 'cancelled' are allowed, which is a requirement in the User Task.",
    "The 'Access System State' Use Case does not explicitly state that the system must provide read-only access to reservations and orders with their statuses, as required by the User Task. While the scenario mentions 'including reservations and orders with their statuses,' it should clarify that this access is read-only to prevent unintended modifications."
  ]
}
===============================


===== OUTPUT_generate_use_case OUTPUT =====
Here’s the revised Use Case with corrections applied strictly based on the valid review issues:

---

### **Use Case: Manage Users in E-Shop Backend**
**Primary Actor:** Administrator
**Goal:** Create or delete users in the system while maintaining consistency of carts, reservations, and stock.

**System Responsibilities:**
- Maintain a registry of users with unique IDs.
- Automatically create an empty cart for each new user.
- Prevent duplicate user creation.
- Handle user deletion by removing their cart, canceling reservations, and updating stock.
- Preserve existing orders of deleted users.

**Preconditions:**
- The system is operational and accessible.

**Postconditions:**
- A new user is created with an empty cart, or an existing user is deleted with their cart and reservations removed.
- Stock is updated if products from canceled reservations still exist.
- **Existing orders of the deleted user remain unaffected in the system.**

**Main Success Scenario (Create User):**
1. Administrator requests to create a user with a unique ID.
2. System validates the ID is not already in use.
3. System creates the user and assigns an empty cart.
4. System confirms successful creation.

**Extensions:**
2a. User ID already exists → System rejects the request and notifies the administrator.
1-4a. Invalid input (e.g., empty ID) → System ignores the request.

**Main Success Scenario (Delete User):**
1. Administrator requests to delete a user by ID.
2. System validates the user exists.
3. System removes the user’s cart and cancels all their reservations.
4. For each canceled reservation:
   - If the product exists in stock, the reserved quantity is returned to available stock.
   - If the product no longer exists, the reservation is removed without stock updates.
5. **System ensures existing orders of the deleted user remain unaffected.**
6. System confirms successful deletion.

**Extensions:**
2a. User does not exist → System ignores the request.
3-6a. System fails to process reservations → No changes are applied (atomicity).

---

### **Use Case: Manage Products in E-Shop Backend**
**Primary Actor:** Administrator
**Goal:** Add or delete products while maintaining stock consistency and preserving existing reservations/orders.

**System Responsibilities:**
- Maintain a product catalog with names, prices, and stock.
- Allow zero stock for products.
- Update existing products if a duplicate name is provided (increase stock, update price).
- Delete products only from available stock, leaving reservations/orders intact.

**Preconditions:**
- The system is operational.

**Postconditions:**
- A new product is added or an existing product is updated/deleted.
- Available stock reflects changes without affecting reservations/orders.
- **Reservations and orders for deleted products remain intact.**

**Main Success Scenario (Add/Update Product):**
1. Administrator requests to add a product with name, price, and stock.
2. System checks if the product name already exists.
3. If the product exists:
   - System increases its stock by the provided quantity.
   - System updates its price to the new value.
4. If the product does not exist:
   - System creates a new product entry.
5. System confirms the operation.

**Extensions:**
1a. Invalid input (e.g., negative price/stock) → System ignores the request.
2-4a. System fails to update stock → No changes are applied.

**Main Success Scenario (Delete Product):**
1. Administrator requests to delete a product by name.
2. System validates the product exists in available stock.
3. System removes the product **only from available stock, leaving reservations and orders unchanged.**
4. **System ensures reservations and orders for the deleted product remain intact.**
5. System confirms deletion.

**Extensions:**
2a. Product does not exist → System ignores the request.
3a. Product has active reservations/orders → System retains them unchanged.

---

### **Use Case: Modify User Cart**
**Primary Actor:** User
**Goal:** Add or remove products from their cart, creating/updating reservations and adjusting stock.

**System Responsibilities:**
- Validate product availability before adding to cart.
- Block reserved quantities from other users.
- Handle cart modifications atomically.
- Return stock if products are removed from the cart.

**Preconditions:**
- The user exists and has an active cart.

**Postconditions:**
- The cart reflects the requested changes.
- Reservations and stock are updated accordingly.
- **Reserved quantities are not available to other users.**

**Main Success Scenario (Add Product to Cart):**
1. User requests to add a product with a specified quantity to their cart.
2. System validates the product exists and the quantity is available in stock.
3. System deducts the quantity from available stock and creates/updates a reservation.
4. System confirms the addition.

**Extensions:**
2a. Product does not exist or quantity is unavailable → System ignores the request.
2b. Requested quantity is zero → System ignores the request.
3a. Product already in cart → System **validates the new quantity is available in stock, then increases the reserved quantity by the new quantity.**

**Main Success Scenario (Remove Product from Cart):**
1. User requests to remove a product with a specified quantity from their cart.
2. System validates the product exists in the cart and the quantity does not exceed the reserved amount.
3. System returns the quantity to available stock (if the product still exists) and updates the reservation.
4. System confirms the removal.

**Extensions:**
2a. Product not in cart or quantity exceeds reservation → System ignores the request.
3a. Product no longer exists in stock → System removes the reservation without stock updates.

---

### **Use Case: Perform Checkout**
**Primary Actor:** User
**Goal:** Convert a cart into an order with atomic success/failure handling.

**System Responsibilities:**
- Validate cart contents and product existence.
- Create an order with a unique sequential ID and price snapshot.
- Clear the cart and reservations on success.
- Ensure atomicity (no partial changes on failure).

**Preconditions:**
- The user has a non-empty cart.

**Postconditions:**
- A new order is created with status "pending," or the checkout fails with no changes.
- **The order ID is globally unique and strictly sequential across the entire system.**

**Main Success Scenario:**
1. User requests to checkout their cart.
2. System validates all reserved products still exist in stock.
3. **If any reserved product no longer exists, the system cancels checkout and notifies the user.**
4. System calculates the total price using current product prices.
5. System **generates a globally unique and strictly sequential order ID (e.g., "SK00001").**
6. System creates an order with status "pending" and stores the price snapshot.
7. System clears the cart and reservations.
8. System confirms the order.

**Extensions:**
4a. Order ID generation fails → System cancels checkout.
7a. System fails to clear cart/reservations → No order is created (atomic rollback).

---

### **Use Case: Update Order Status**
**Primary Actor:** Administrator
**Goal:** Transition an order’s status while enforcing valid sequences and handling cancellations.

**System Responsibilities:**
- Enforce valid status transitions (e.g., pending → processing → completed).
- Allow cancellation from any state, including "delivered."
- Restore stock for canceled orders (recreate products if necessary).
- **Ignore further status changes if the order is in a terminal state (e.g., "cancelled").**

**Preconditions:**
- The order exists.

**Postconditions:**
- The order’s status is updated, or the request is ignored.
- **If the order is cancelled, it becomes a terminal state, and further status changes are ignored.**

**Main Success Scenario:**
1. Administrator requests to update an order’s status.
2. System validates the transition is allowed (e.g., pending → processing, or any state → cancelled).
3. **If the status is set to "cancelled":**
   - For each product in the order:
     - If the product exists, increase its stock by the ordered quantity.
     - If the product no longer exists, **recreate it with the price stored in the order.**
4. **If the order is already in a terminal state (e.g., "cancelled"), the system ignores the request.**
5. System updates the status.
6. System confirms the update.

**Extensions:**
2a. Invalid transition (e.g., delivered → processing, unless transitioning to "cancelled") → System ignores the request.
3a. Transition to "cancelled":
   - For each product in the order:
     - If the product exists, increase its stock by the ordered quantity.
     - If the product no longer exists, recreate it with the price stored in the order.
4a. Order is already "cancelled" → System ignores further status changes.

---

### **Use Case: Access System State**
**Primary Actor:** Administrator/User
**Goal:** Retrieve current system state (users, products, stock, carts, orders).

**System Responsibilities:**
- Provide read-only access to:
  - Current users and their carts.
  - Products (name, price, available stock).
  - Reservations and orders with statuses.

**Preconditions:**
- The system is operational.

**Postconditions:**
- The requested state is returned without modifications.

**Main Success Scenario:**
1. Actor requests system state (e.g., list of users).
2. System **provides read-only access to the data, including reservations and orders with their statuses.**

**Extensions:**
1a. Invalid request (e.g., non-existent entity) → System returns an empty or error response.

---

{
  "score": 0.85,
  "issues": [
    "Missing explicit handling of 'terminal state' in `update_order_status` method steps. The method description mentions ignoring further status changes for 'cancelled' orders, but the steps do not explicitly include this check, which could lead to inconsistent behavior.",
    "The `checkout` method does not explicitly state in its steps that it must validate all reserved products still exist in stock before proceeding. While the special requirements mention this, the steps should reflect this critical validation for atomicity.",
    "The `update_order_status` method does not explicitly state in its steps that it must recreate products with the price stored in the order if the product no longer exists. This is only mentioned in special requirements, not in the steps.",
    "The `delete_user` method steps do not explicitly mention that existing orders of the deleted user must remain unaffected, which is a critical requirement. This is only mentioned in special requirements, not in the steps.",
    "The `add_to_cart` method does not explicitly state in its steps that reserved quantities must not be available to other users, which is a key requirement for stock consistency.",
    "The `checkout` method steps do not explicitly mention that the order ID must be globally unique and strictly sequential, which is a critical requirement for order management.",
    "The `update_order_status` method steps do not explicitly enforce the allowed status transitions (e.g., pending -> processing -> completed). This is only mentioned in special requirements, not in the steps."
  ]

  "issues": [
    "The `checkout` method does not explicitly state that it must validate that all reserved products still exist in the stock system before proceeding. While the special requirements mention this, the steps do not reflect this critical validation.",
    "The `checkout` method does not include a step to generate a globally unique and strictly sequential order ID (e.g., 'SK00001'). The special requirements mention this, but the steps do not reflect the generation or assignment of the ID.",
    "The `update_order_status` method does not explicitly state that if an order is already in a terminal state (e.g., 'cancelled'), further status changes must be ignored. This is mentioned in special requirements but not in the steps.",
    "The `update_order_status` method does not include steps to recreate products with the price stored in the order if the product no longer exists when canceling an order. This is mentioned in special requirements but not in the steps.",
    "The `delete_user` method does not explicitly state that existing orders of the deleted user must remain unaffected. While this is mentioned in special requirements, it is not reflected in the steps.",
    "The `add_to_cart` method does not explicitly state that adding a product already in the cart should validate the new quantity is available in stock before increasing the reserved quantity. This is implied but not clearly stated in the steps.",
    "The `remove_from_cart` method does not explicitly state that if the product no longer exists in the stock system, the reservation should be removed without affecting stock. This is mentioned in special requirements but not in the steps."
  ]

def create_user(self, user_id):
    if not user_id or user_id in self.users:
        return False

    self.users[user_id] = {
        'cart': {},
        'reservations': {}
    }
    return True

===== generate_single_method OUTPUT =====
Generated code for EShopSystem.delete_user: 
def delete_user(self, user_id):
    if user_id not in self.users:
        return False

    # Process reservations
    for product_name, quantity in self.users[user_id]['reservations'].items():
        if product_name in self.products:
            self.products[product_name]['stock'] += quantity

    # Clear cart and reservations
    self.users[user_id]['cart'].clear()
    self.users[user_id]['reservations'].clear()

    # Remove user
    del self.users[user_id]

    return True

def add_product(self, product_name, price, stock):
    if not product_name or price < 0 or stock < 0:
        return False

    if product_name in self.products:
        self.products[product_name]['stock'] += stock
        self.products[product_name]['price'] = price
    else:
        self.products[product_name] = {
            'price': price,
            'stock': stock
        }

    return True

===== generate_single_method OUTPUT =====
Generated code for EShopSystem.add_to_cart: 
@classmethod
def add_to_cart(cls, user_id, product_name, quantity):
    # Validate inputs
    if quantity <= 0:
        return False

    # Check if user exists
    if user_id not in cls.users:
        return False

    # Check if product exists and has sufficient stock
    if product_name not in cls.products:
        return False

    product = cls.products[product_name]
    available_stock = product['stock']

    # Check if product is already in cart
    if product_name in cls.users[user_id]['cart']:
        current_quantity = cls.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name]
        total_quantity = current_quantity + quantity
    else:
        total_quantity = quantity

    # Check if sufficient stock is available
    if total_quantity > available_stock:
        return False

    # Deduct from available stock
    cls.products[product_name]['stock'] -= quantity

    # Update user's cart
    if product_name in cls.users[user_id]['cart']:
        cls.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] += quantity
    else:
        cls.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] = quantity

    # Update user's reservation
    if product_name in cls.users[user_id]['reservations']:
        cls.users[user_id]['reservations'][product_name] += quantity
    else:
        cls.users[user_id]['reservations'][product_name] = quantity

    return True
===============================


===== generate_single_method OUTPUT =====
Generated code for EShopSystem.remove_from_cart: 
def remove_from_cart(self, user_id, product_name, quantity):
    # Validate user exists and product is in cart with sufficient quantity
    if user_id not in self.users or product_name not in self.users[user_id]['cart']:
        return False

    if self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] < quantity:
        return False

    # Check if product exists in stock
    product_exists = product_name in self.products

    # Return quantity to stock if product exists
    if product_exists:
        self.products[product_name]['stock'] += quantity

    # Update user's cart
    if self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] == quantity:
        del self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name]
    else:
        self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] -= quantity

    # Update user's reservation
    if product_exists:
        if product_name in self.users[user_id]['reservations']:
            if self.users[user_id]['reservations'][product_name] == quantity:
                del self.users[user_id]['reservations'][product_name]
            else:
                self.users[user_id]['reservations'][product_name] -= quantity
    else:
        if product_name in self.users[user_id]['reservations']:
            del self.users[user_id]['reservations'][product_name]

    return True

===== generate_single_method OUTPUT =====
Generated code for EShopSystem.checkout: 
def checkout(self, user_id):
    # Step 1: Validate user exists and has non-empty cart
    if user_id not in self.users or not self.users[user_id]['cart']:
        return None

    cart = self.users[user_id]['cart']
    price_snapshot = {}
    total_price = 0.0

    # Step 2: Validate all reserved products still exist
    for product_name, quantity in cart.items():
        if product_name not in self.products:
            return None

        # Step 4: Calculate total price using current prices
        price_snapshot[product_name] = self.products[product_name]['price']
        total_price += self.products[product_name]['price'] * quantity

    # Step 5: Generate unique sequential order ID
    order_id = f"SK{self.next_order_id:05d}"
    self.next_order_id += 1

    # Step 6: Create order with status 'pending'
    order_data = {
        'status': 'pending',
        'items': cart.copy(),
        'price_snapshot': price_snapshot,
        'total_price': total_price
    }
    self.orders[order_id] = order_data

    # Step 7: Clear user's cart and reservations
    self.users[user_id]['cart'].clear()
    if user_id in self.reservations:
        self.reservations[user_id].clear()

    # Step 8: Add order to orders registry
    return order_id
===============================


===== generate_single_method OUTPUT =====
Generated code for EShopSystem.update_order_status: 
def update_order_status(self, order_id, new_status):
    # Validate order exists
    if order_id not in self.orders:
        return False

    current_status = self.orders[order_id]['status']

    # Check if order is already in terminal state
    if current_status == 'cancelled':
        return False

    # Validate status transition
    valid_transitions = {
        'pending': ['processing', 'cancelled'],
        'processing': ['completed', 'cancelled'],
        'completed': ['shipped', 'cancelled'],
        'shipped': ['delivered', 'cancelled'],
        'delivered': ['cancelled']
    }

    if new_status not in valid_transitions.get(current_status, []):
        return False

    # Handle cancellation
    if new_status == 'cancelled':
        for product_name, quantity in self.orders[order_id]['items'].items():
            if product_name in self.products:
                # Restore stock if product exists
                self.products[product_name]['stock'] += quantity
            else:
                # Recreate product with price from order snapshot
                price = self.orders[order_id]['price_snapshot'].get(product_name, 0.0)
                self.products[product_name] = {
                    'price': price,
                    'stock': quantity
                }

    # Update order status
    self.orders[order_id]['status'] = new_status
    return True
===============================


===== generate_single_method OUTPUT =====
Generated code for EShopSystem.get_system_state: 
def get_system_state(self):
    """Retrieves the current system state, including users, products, stock, carts, and orders.

    Returns:
        dict: A read-only snapshot of the current system state containing:
            - users: Dictionary of user data including carts and reservations
            - products: Dictionary of product data including price and stock
            - orders: Dictionary of order data including status, items, and price snapshot
            - next_order_id: Current next order ID counter
            - reservations: Dictionary of product reservations by user
    """
    return {
        'users': self.users.copy(),
        'products': self.products.copy(),
        'orders': self.orders.copy(),
        'next_order_id': self.next_order_id,
        'reservations': self.reservations.copy()
    }

Regenerated code for EShopSystem.delete_user: 
def delete_user(self, user_id):
    if user_id not in self.users:
        return False

    # Process reservations
    reservations = self.users[user_id].get('reservations', {})
    for product_name, quantity in reservations.items():
        if product_name in self.products:
            self.products[product_name]['stock'] += quantity

    # Clear cart and reservations
    self.users[user_id]['cart'].clear()
    self.users[user_id].get('reservations', {}).clear()

    # Remove user from reservations tracking
    if user_id in self.reservations:
        del self.reservations[user_id]

    # Remove user
    del self.users[user_id]

    return True

===== regenerate_single_method OUTPUT =====
Regenerated code for EShopSystem.add_to_cart: 
@classmethod
def add_to_cart(cls, user_id, product_name, quantity):
    # Validate inputs
    if quantity <= 0:
        return False

    # Check if user exists
    if user_id not in cls.users:
        return False

    # Check if product exists and has sufficient stock
    if product_name not in cls.products:
        return False

    product = cls.products[product_name]
    available_stock = product['stock']

    # Check if product is already in cart
    if product_name in cls.users[user_id]['cart']:
        current_quantity = cls.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name]
        total_quantity = current_quantity + quantity
    else:
        total_quantity = quantity

    # Check if sufficient stock is available
    if total_quantity > available_stock:
        return False

    # Deduct from available stock
    cls.products[product_name]['stock'] -= quantity

    # Update user's cart
    if product_name in cls.users[user_id]['cart']:
        cls.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] += quantity
    else:
        cls.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] = quantity

    # Update user's reservation
    if product_name in cls.users[user_id]['reservations']:
        cls.users[user_id]['reservations'][product_name] += quantity
    else:
        cls.users[user_id]['reservations'][product_name] = quantity

    # Update global reservations
    if user_id in cls.reservations:
        if product_name in cls.reservations[user_id]:
            cls.reservations[user_id][product_name] += quantity
        else:
            cls.reservations[user_id][product_name] = quantity
    else:
        cls.reservations[user_id] = {product_name: quantity}

    return True
===============================

def remove_from_cart(self, user_id, product_name, quantity):
    # Validate user exists and product is in cart with sufficient quantity
    if user_id not in self.users or product_name not in self.users[user_id]['cart']:
        return False

    if self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] < quantity:
        return False

    # Check if product exists in stock
    product_exists = product_name in self.products

    # Return quantity to stock if product exists
    if product_exists:
        self.products[product_name]['stock'] += quantity

    # Update user's cart
    if self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] == quantity:
        del self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name]
    else:
        self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] -= quantity

    # Update user's reservation

            del self.users[user_id]['reservations'][product_name]
        else:
            self.users[user_id]['reservations'][product_name] -= quantity

    return True

===== regenerate_single_method OUTPUT =====
Regenerated code for EShopSystem.checkout: 
def checkout(self, user_id):
    # Step 1: Validate user exists and has non-empty cart
    if user_id not in self.users or not self.users[user_id]['cart']:
        return None

    cart = self.users[user_id]['cart']
    price_snapshot = {}
    total_price = 0.0

    # Step 2: Validate all reserved products still exist and have sufficient stock
    for product_name, quantity in cart.items():
        if product_name not in self.products or self.products[product_name]['stock'] < quantity:
            return None

        # Step 4: Calculate total price using current prices
        price_snapshot[product_name] = self.products[product_name]['price']
        total_price += self.products[product_name]['price'] * quantity

    # Step 5: Generate unique sequential order ID
    order_id = f"SK{self.next_order_id:05d}"

    # Step 6: Create order with status 'pending'
    order_data = {
        'status': 'pending',
        'items': cart.copy(),
        'price_snapshot': price_snapshot,
        'total_price': total_price
    }

    # Step 7: Clear user's cart and reservations
    self.users[user_id]['cart'].clear()
    if user_id in self.reservations:
        self.reservations[user_id].clear()

    # Step 8: Add order to orders registry and increment order ID
    self.orders[order_id] = order_data
    self.next_order_id += 1

    return order_id
===============================


===== regenerate_single_method OUTPUT =====
Regenerated code for EShopSystem.get_system_state: 
def get_system_state(self):
    """Retrieves the current system state, including users, products, stock, carts, and orders.

    Returns:
        dict: A read-only snapshot of the current system state containing:
            - users: Dictionary of user data including carts and reservations
            - products: Dictionary of product data including price and stock
            - orders: Dictionary of order data including status, items, and price snapshot
            - next_order_id: Current next order ID counter
            - reservations: Dictionary of product reservations by user
    """
    from copy import deepcopy
    return {
        'users': deepcopy(self.users),
        'products': deepcopy(self.products),
        'orders': deepcopy(self.orders),
        'next_order_id': self.next_order_id,
        'reservations': deepcopy(self.reservations)
    }

Regenerated code for EShopSystem.delete_user: 
def delete_user(self, user_id):
    if user_id not in self.users:
        return False

    # Process reservations
    reservations = self.users[user_id].get('reservations', {})
    for product_name, quantity in reservations.items():
        if product_name in self.products:
            self.products[product_name]['stock'] += quantity

    # Clear cart and reservations
    if 'cart' in self.users[user_id]:
        self.users[user_id]['cart'].clear()
    if 'reservations' in self.users[user_id]:
        self.users[user_id]['reservations'].clear()

    # Remove user from reservations tracking
    if user_id in self.reservations:
        del self.reservations[user_id]

    # Remove user
    del self.users[user_id]

    return True

===== regenerate_single_method OUTPUT =====
Regenerated code for EShopSystem.add_to_cart: 
@classmethod
def add_to_cart(cls, user_id, product_name, quantity):
    # Validate inputs
    if quantity <= 0:
        return False

    # Check if user exists
    if user_id not in cls.users:
        return False

    # Check if product exists and has sufficient stock
    if product_name not in cls.products:
        return False

    product = cls.products[product_name]
    available_stock = product['stock']

    # Calculate total quantity including existing cart items
    if product_name in cls.users[user_id]['cart']:
        current_quantity = cls.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name]
        total_quantity = current_quantity + quantity
    else:
        total_quantity = quantity

    # Check if sufficient stock is available
    if total_quantity > available_stock:
        return False

    # Deduct from available stock
    cls.products[product_name]['stock'] -= quantity

    # Update user's cart
    if product_name in cls.users[user_id]['cart']:
        cls.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] += quantity
    else:
        cls.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] = quantity

    # Update user's reservation
    if product_name in cls.users[user_id]['reservations']:
        cls.users[user_id]['reservations'][product_name] += quantity
    else:
        cls.users[user_id]['reservations'][product_name] = quantity

    # Update global reservations
    if user_id in cls.reservations:
        if product_name in cls.reservations[user_id]:
            cls.reservations[user_id][product_name] += quantity
        else:
            cls.reservations[user_id][product_name] = quantity
    else:
        cls.reservations[user_id] = {product_name: quantity}

    return True
===============================


===== regenerate_single_method OUTPUT =====
Regenerated code for EShopSystem.remove_from_cart: 
def remove_from_cart(self, user_id, product_name, quantity):
    # Validate user exists and product is in cart with sufficient quantity
    if user_id not in self.users or product_name not in self.users[user_id]['cart']:
        return False

    if quantity <= 0 or self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] < quantity:
        return False

    # Check if product exists in stock
    product_exists = product_name in self.products

    # Return quantity to stock if product exists
    if product_exists:
        self.products[product_name]['stock'] += quantity

    # Update user's cart
    if self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] == quantity:
        del self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name]
    else:
        self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] -= quantity

    # Ensure reservations dictionary exists
    if 'reservations' not in self.users[user_id]:
        self.users[user_id]['reservations'] = {}

    # Update user's reservation
    if product_name in self.users[user_id]['reservations']:
        if self.users[user_id]['reservations'][product_name] == quantity:
            del self.users[user_id]['reservations'][product_name]
        else:
            self.users[user_id]['reservations'][product_name] -= quantity

    return True

===== regenerate_single_method OUTPUT =====
Regenerated code for EShopSystem.checkout: 
def checkout(self, user_id):
    # Step 1: Validate user exists and has non-empty cart
    if user_id not in self.users or not self.users[user_id]['cart']:
        return None

    cart = self.users[user_id]['cart']
    price_snapshot = {}
    total_price = 0.0

    # Step 2: Validate all reserved products still exist and have sufficient stock
    for product_name, quantity in cart.items():
        if product_name not in self.products or self.products[product_name]['stock'] < quantity:
            return None

        # Step 4: Calculate total price using current prices
        price_snapshot[product_name] = self.products[product_name]['price']
        total_price += self.products[product_name]['price'] * quantity

    # Step 5: Generate unique sequential order ID
    order_id = f"SK{self.next_order_id:05d}"

    # Step 6: Create order with status 'pending'
    order_data = {
        'status': 'pending',
        'items': cart.copy(),
        'price_snapshot': price_snapshot,
        'total_price': total_price
    }

    # Step 7: Clear user's cart and reservations
    self.users[user_id]['cart'].clear()
    if user_id in self.reservations:
        self.reservations[user_id].clear()

    # Step 8: Add order to orders registry and increment order ID
    self.orders[order_id] = order_data
    self.next_order_id += 1

    return order_id
===============================


===== regenerate_single_method OUTPUT =====
Regenerated code for EShopSystem.get_system_state: 
def get_system_state(self):
    """Retrieves the current system state, including users, products, stock, carts, and orders.

    Returns:
        dict: A read-only snapshot of the current system state containing:
            - users: Dictionary of user data including carts and reservations
            - products: Dictionary of product data including price and stock
            - orders: Dictionary of order data including status, items, and price snapshot
            - next_order_id: Current next order ID counter
            - reservations: Dictionary of product reservations by user
    """
    from copy import deepcopy
    from types import MappingProxyType

    state = {
        'users': deepcopy(self.users),
        'products': deepcopy(self.products),
        'orders': deepcopy(self.orders),
        'next_order_id': self.next_order_id,
        'reservations': deepcopy(self.reservations)
    }
    return MappingProxyType(state)

def delete_user(self, user_id):
    if user_id not in self.users:
        return False

    # Remove user from reservations tracking first
    if user_id in self.reservations:
        del self.reservations[user_id]

    # Process reservations
    reservations = self.users[user_id].get('reservations', {})
    for product_name, quantity in reservations.items():
        if product_name in self.products:
            self.products[product_name]['stock'] += quantity

    # Remove cart and reservations
    if 'cart' in self.users[user_id]:
        del self.users[user_id]['cart']
    if 'reservations' in self.users[user_id]:
        del self.users[user_id]['reservations']

    # Remove user
    del self.users[user_id]

    return True

Regenerated code for EShopSystem.add_to_cart: 
@classmethod
def add_to_cart(cls, user_id, product_name, quantity):
    # Validate inputs
    if quantity <= 0:
        return False

    # Check if user exists
    if user_id not in cls.users:
        return False

    # Check if product exists
    if product_name not in cls.products:
        return False

    product = cls.products[product_name]
    available_stock = product['stock']

    # Calculate total reserved quantity for this product across all users
    total_reserved = 0
    for user_reservations in cls.reservations.values():
        if product_name in user_reservations:
            total_reserved += user_reservations[product_name]

    # Calculate total quantity including existing cart items
    if product_name in cls.users[user_id]['cart']:
        current_quantity = cls.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name]
        total_quantity = current_quantity + quantity
    else:
        total_quantity = quantity

    # Check if sufficient stock is available (considering all reservations)
    if total_quantity > (available_stock - total_reserved):
        return False

    # Deduct from available stock
    cls.products[product_name]['stock'] -= quantity

    # Update user's cart
    if product_name in cls.users[user_id]['cart']:
        cls.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] += quantity
    else:
        cls.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] = quantity

    # Update user's reservation
    if product_name in cls.users[user_id]['reservations']:
        cls.users[user_id]['reservations'][product_name] += quantity
    else:
        cls.users[user_id]['reservations'][product_name] = quantity

    # Update global reservations
    if user_id in cls.reservations:
        if product_name in cls.reservations[user_id]:
            cls.reservations[user_id][product_name] += quantity
        else:
            cls.reservations[user_id][product_name] = quantity
    else:
        cls.reservations[user_id] = {product_name: quantity}

    return True

def remove_from_cart(self, user_id, product_name, quantity):
    # Validate user exists and product is in cart with sufficient quantity
    if user_id not in self.users or product_name not in self.users[user_id]['cart']:
        return False

    if quantity <= 0 or self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] < quantity:
        return False

    # Check if product exists in stock
    product_exists = product_name in self.products

    # Return quantity to stock if product exists
    if product_exists:
        self.products[product_name]['stock'] += quantity

    # Update user's cart
    if self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] == quantity:
        del self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name]
    else:
        self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] -= quantity

                del self.reservations[user_id]
        else:
            self.reservations[user_id][product_name] -= quantity

    return True

def add_to_cart(self, user_id, product_name, quantity):
    # Validate inputs
    if quantity <= 0:
        return False

    # Check if user exists
    if user_id not in self.users:
        return False

    # Check if product exists
    if product_name not in self.products:
        return False

    product = self.products[product_name]
    available_stock = product['stock']

    # Calculate total reserved quantity for this product across all users
    total_reserved = 0
    for user_reservations in self.reservations.values():
        if product_name in user_reservations:
            total_reserved += user_reservations[product_name]

    # Calculate total quantity including existing cart items
    if product_name in self.users[user_id]['cart']:
        current_quantity = self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name]
        total_quantity = current_quantity + quantity
    else:
        total_quantity = quantity

    # Check if sufficient stock is available (considering all reservations)
    if total_quantity > (available_stock - total_reserved):
        return False

    # Deduct from available stock
    self.products[product_name]['stock'] -= quantity

    # Update user's cart
    if product_name in self.users[user_id]['cart']:
        self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] += quantity
    else:
        self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] = quantity

    # Update user's reservation
    if product_name in self.users[user_id]['reservations']:
        self.users[user_id]['reservations'][product_name] += quantity
    else:
        self.users[user_id]['reservations'][product_name] = quantity

    # Update global reservations
    if user_id in self.reservations:
        if product_name in self.reservations[user_id]:
            self.reservations[user_id][product_name] += quantity
        else:
            self.reservations[user_id][product_name] = quantity
    else:
        self.reservations[user_id] = {product_name: quantity}

    return True

def remove_from_cart(self, user_id, product_name, quantity):
    # Validate user exists and product is in cart with sufficient quantity
    if user_id not in self.users or product_name not in self.users[user_id]['cart']:
        return False

    if quantity <= 0 or self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] < quantity:
        return False

    # Check if product exists in stock
    product_exists = product_name in self.products

    # Return quantity to stock if product exists
    if product_exists:
        self.products[product_name]['stock'] += quantity

    # Update user's cart
    if self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] == quantity:
        del self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name]
    else:
        self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] -= quantity

    # Update user's reservations
    if user_id in self.users and 'reservations' in self.users[user_id] and product_name in self.users[user_id]['reservations']:
        if self.users[user_id]['reservations'][product_name] == quantity:
            del self.users[user_id]['reservations'][product_name]
            if not self.users[user_id]['reservations']:  # Clean up empty reservations entry
                del self.users[user_id]['reservations']
        else:
            self.users[user_id]['reservations'][product_name] -= quantity

    return True

def checkout(self, user_id):
    # Step 1: Validate user exists and has non-empty cart
    if user_id not in self.users or not self.users[user_id]['cart']:
        return None

    cart = self.users[user_id]['cart']
    price_snapshot = {}
    total_price = 0.0

    # Step 2: Validate all reserved products still exist and have sufficient stock
    for product_name, quantity in cart.items():
        if product_name not in self.products or self.products[product_name]['stock'] < quantity:
            return None

        # Step 4: Calculate total price using current prices
        price_snapshot[product_name] = self.products[product_name]['price']
        total_price += self.products[product_name]['price'] * quantity

    # Step 5: Generate unique sequential order ID
    order_id = f"SK{self.next_order_id:05d}"

    # Step 6: Create order with status 'pending'
    order_data = {
        'status': 'pending',
        'items': cart.copy(),
        'price_snapshot': price_snapshot,
        'total_price': total_price
    }

    # Step 7: Clear user's cart and reservations
    self.users[user_id]['cart'].clear()
    if user_id in self.users[user_id]['reservations']:
        self.users[user_id]['reservations'].clear()
    if user_id in self.reservations:
        self.reservations[user_id].clear()

    # Step 8: Add order to orders registry and increment order ID
    self.orders[order_id] = order_data
    self.next_order_id += 1

    return order_id

===== regenerate_single_method OUTPUT =====
Regenerated code for EShopSystem.update_order_status: 
def update_order_status(self, order_id, new_status):
    # Validate order exists
    if order_id not in self.orders:
        return False

    current_status = self.orders[order_id]['status']

    # Check if order is already in terminal state
    if current_status == 'cancelled':
        return False

    # Validate status transition
    valid_transitions = {
        'pending': ['processing', 'cancelled'],
        'processing': ['completed', 'cancelled'],
        'completed': ['shipped', 'cancelled'],
        'shipped': ['delivered', 'cancelled'],
        'delivered': ['cancelled']
    }

    # Check if the transition is valid
    if new_status not in valid_transitions.get(current_status, []):
        return False

    # Handle cancellation
    if new_status == 'cancelled':
        for product_name, quantity in self.orders[order_id]['items'].items():
            if product_name in self.products:
                # Restore stock if product exists
                self.products[product_name]['stock'] += quantity
            else:
                # Recreate product with price from order snapshot
                price = self.orders[order_id]['price_snapshot'].get(product_name, 0.0)
                self.products[product_name] = {
                    'price': price,
                    'stock': quantity
                }

    # Update order status
    self.orders[order_id]['status'] = new_status
    return True

Regenerated code for EShopSystem.delete_user: 
def delete_user(self, user_id):
    if user_id not in self.users:
        return False

    # Process reservations
    reservations = self.users[user_id].get('reservations', {})
    for product_name, quantity in reservations.items():
        if product_name in self.products:
            self.products[product_name]['stock'] += quantity

    # Remove user from reservations tracking
    if user_id in self.reservations:
        del self.reservations[user_id]

    # Remove user's reservations from their own data
    if 'reservations' in self.users[user_id]:
        del self.users[user_id]['reservations']

    # Remove user
    del self.users[user_id]

    return True

===== regenerate_single_method OUTPUT =====
Regenerated code for EShopSystem.add_to_cart: 
def add_to_cart(self, user_id, product_name, quantity):
    # Validate inputs
    if quantity <= 0:
        return False

    # Check if user exists
    if user_id not in self.users:
        return False

    # Check if product exists
    if product_name not in self.products:
        return False

    product = self.products[product_name]
    available_stock = product['stock']

    # Calculate total reserved quantity for this product across all users
    total_reserved = 0
    for user_reservations in self.reservations.values():
        if product_name in user_reservations:
            total_reserved += user_reservations[product_name]

    # Calculate total quantity including existing cart items
    if product_name in self.users[user_id]['cart']:
        current_quantity = self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name]
        total_quantity = current_quantity + quantity
    else:
        total_quantity = quantity

    # Check if sufficient stock is available (considering all reservations)
    if total_quantity > (available_stock - total_reserved):
        return False

    # Deduct from available stock
    self.products[product_name]['stock'] -= quantity

    # Update user's cart
    if product_name in self.users[user_id]['cart']:
        self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] += quantity
    else:
        self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] = quantity

    # Update user's reservation
    if user_id in self.reservations:
        if product_name in self.reservations[user_id]:
            self.reservations[user_id][product_name] += quantity
        else:
            self.reservations[user_id][product_name] = quantity
    else:
        self.reservations[user_id] = {product_name: quantity}

    return True
===============================


===== regenerate_single_method OUTPUT =====
Regenerated code for EShopSystem.remove_from_cart: 
def remove_from_cart(self, user_id, product_name, quantity):
    # Validate user exists and product is in cart with sufficient quantity
    if user_id not in self.users or product_name not in self.users[user_id]['cart']:
        return False

    if quantity <= 0 or self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] < quantity:
        return False

    # Check if product exists in stock
    product_exists = product_name in self.products

    # Return quantity to stock if product exists
    if product_exists:
        self.products[product_name]['stock'] += quantity

    # Update user's cart
    if self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] == quantity:
        del self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name]
    else:
        self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] -= quantity

    # Update user's reservations
    if product_name in self.users[user_id].get('reservations', {}):
        if self.users[user_id]['reservations'][product_name] == quantity:
            del self.users[user_id]['reservations'][product_name]
            if not self.users[user_id]['reservations']:  # Clean up empty reservations entry
                del self.users[user_id]['reservations']
        else:
            self.users[user_id]['reservations'][product_name] -= quantity

    # Update global reservations
    if user_id in self.reservations and product_name in self.reservations[user_id]:
        if self.reservations[user_id][product_name] == quantity:
            del self.reservations[user_id][product_name]
            if not self.reservations[user_id]:  # Clean up empty reservations entry
                del self.reservations[user_id]
        else:
            self.reservations[user_id][product_name] -= quantity

    return True

===== regenerate_single_method OUTPUT =====
Regenerated code for EShopSystem.checkout: 
def checkout(self, user_id):
    # Step 1: Validate user exists and has non-empty cart
    if user_id not in self.users or not self.users[user_id]['cart']:
        return None

    cart = self.users[user_id]['cart']
    price_snapshot = {}
    total_price = 0.0

    # Step 2: Validate all reserved products still exist in the stock system
    for product_name in cart.keys():
        if product_name not in self.products:
            return None

    # Step 3: Validate all reserved products have sufficient stock
    for product_name, quantity in cart.items():
        if self.products[product_name]['stock'] < quantity:
            return None

        # Step 4: Calculate total price using current prices
        price_snapshot[product_name] = self.products[product_name]['price']
        total_price += self.products[product_name]['price'] * quantity

    # Step 5: Generate unique sequential order ID
    order_id = f"SK{self.next_order_id:05d}"

    # Step 6: Create order with status 'pending'
    order_data = {
        'status': 'pending',
        'items': cart.copy(),
        'price_snapshot': price_snapshot,
        'total_price': total_price
    }

    # Step 7: Clear user's cart and reservations
    self.users[user_id]['cart'].clear()
    if user_id in self.reservations:
        self.reservations[user_id].clear()

    # Step 8: Add order to orders registry and increment order ID
    self.orders[order_id] = order_data
    self.next_order_id += 1

    return order_id
===============================


===== regenerate_single_method OUTPUT =====
Regenerated code for EShopSystem.update_order_status: 
def update_order_status(self, order_id, new_status):
    # Validate order exists
    if order_id not in self.orders:
        return False

    current_status = self.orders[order_id]['status']

    # Check if order is already in terminal state
    if current_status == 'cancelled':
        return False

    # Validate status transition
    valid_transitions = {
        'pending': ['processing', 'cancelled'],
        'processing': ['completed', 'cancelled'],
        'completed': ['shipped', 'cancelled'],
        'shipped': ['delivered', 'cancelled'],
        'delivered': ['cancelled']
    }

    # Check if the transition is valid
    if new_status not in valid_transitions.get(current_status, []):
        return False

    # Handle cancellation
    if new_status == 'cancelled':
        for product_name, quantity in self.orders[order_id]['items'].items():
            if product_name in self.products:
                # Restore stock if product exists
                self.products[product_name]['stock'] += quantity
            else:
                # Recreate product with price from order snapshot
                price = self.orders[order_id]['price_snapshot'].get(product_name, 0.0)
                self.products[product_name] = {
                    'price': price,
                    'stock': quantity
                }

    # Update order status
    self.orders[order_id]['status'] = new_status
    return True

Regenerated code for EShopSystem.remove_from_cart: 
def remove_from_cart(self, user_id, product_name, quantity):
    # Validate user exists and product is in cart with sufficient quantity
    if user_id not in self.users or product_name not in self.users[user_id]['cart']:
        return False

    if quantity <= 0 or self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] < quantity:
        return False

    # Check if product exists in stock
    product_exists = product_name in self.products

    # Return quantity to stock if product exists
    if product_exists:
        self.products[product_name]['stock'] += quantity

    # Update user's cart
    if self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] == quantity:
        del self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name]
    else:
        self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] -= quantity

    # Update global reservations
    if user_id in self.reservations and product_name in self.reservations[user_id]:
        if self.reservations[user_id][product_name] == quantity:
            del self.reservations[user_id][product_name]
            if not self.reservations[user_id]:  # Clean up empty reservations entry
                del self.reservations[user_id]
        else:
            self.reservations[user_id][product_name] -= quantity

    # Update user's reservations to match global reservations
    if user_id in self.reservations and product_name in self.reservations[user_id]:
        if 'reservations' not in self.users[user_id]:
            self.users[user_id]['reservations'] = {}
        self.users[user_id]['reservations'][product_name] = self.reservations[user_id][product_name]
    else:
        if 'reservations' in self.users[user_id] and product_name in self.users[user_id]['reservations']:
            del self.users[user_id]['reservations'][product_name]
            if not self.users[user_id]['reservations']:
                del self.users[user_id]['reservations']

    return True
