===== assemble_program OUTPUT =====
class EShopSystem:
    def __init__(self):
        self.users = {}
        self.products = {}
        self.orders = {}
        self.next_order_id = 1
        self.reservations = {}

    def create_user(self, user_id):
        if not user_id or user_id in self.users:
            return False

        self.users[user_id] = {
            'cart': {},
            'reservations': {}
        }
        return True

    def add_product(self, product_name, price, stock):
        if not product_name or price < 0 or stock < 0:
            return False

        if product_name in self.products:
            self.products[product_name]['stock'] += stock
            self.products[product_name]['price'] = price
        else:
            self.products[product_name] = {
                'price': price,
                'stock': stock
            }

        return True

    def delete_product(self, product_name):
        if product_name not in self.products:
            return False

        del self.products[product_name]
        return True

    def get_system_state(self):
        """Retrieves the current system state, including users, products, stock, carts, and orders.

        Returns:
            dict: A read-only snapshot of the current system state containing:
                - users: Dictionary of user data including carts and reservations
                - products: Dictionary of product data including price and stock
                - orders: Dictionary of order data including status, items, and price snapshot
                - next_order_id: Current next order ID counter
                - reservations: Dictionary of product reservations by user
        """
        from copy import deepcopy
        from types import MappingProxyType

        state = {
            'users': deepcopy(self.users),
            'products': deepcopy(self.products),
            'orders': deepcopy(self.orders),
            'next_order_id': self.next_order_id,
            'reservations': deepcopy(self.reservations)
        }
        return MappingProxyType(state)

    def delete_user(self, user_id):
        if user_id not in self.users:
            return False

        # Process reservations
        reservations = self.users[user_id].get('reservations', {})
        for product_name, quantity in reservations.items():
            if product_name in self.products:
                self.products[product_name]['stock'] += quantity

        # Remove user from reservations tracking
        if user_id in self.reservations:
            del self.reservations[user_id]

        # Remove user
        del self.users[user_id]

        return True

    def add_to_cart(self, user_id, product_name, quantity):
        # Validate inputs
        if quantity <= 0:
            return False

        # Check if user exists
        if user_id not in self.users:
            return False

        # Check if product exists
        if product_name not in self.products:
            return False

        product = self.products[product_name]
        available_stock = product['stock']

        # Calculate total reserved quantity for this product across all users
        total_reserved = 0
        for user_reservations in self.reservations.values():
            if product_name in user_reservations:
                total_reserved += user_reservations[product_name]

        # Calculate total quantity including existing cart items
        if product_name in self.users[user_id]['cart']:
            current_quantity = self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name]
            total_quantity = current_quantity + quantity
        else:
            total_quantity = quantity

        # Check if sufficient stock is available (considering all reservations)
        if total_quantity > (available_stock - total_reserved):
            return False

        # Update user's cart
        if product_name in self.users[user_id]['cart']:
            self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] += quantity
        else:
            self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] = quantity

        # Deduct from available stock
        self.products[product_name]['stock'] -= quantity

        # Update user's reservation
        if user_id in self.reservations:
            if product_name in self.reservations[user_id]:
                self.reservations[user_id][product_name] += quantity
            else:
                self.reservations[user_id][product_name] = quantity
        else:
            self.reservations[user_id] = {product_name: quantity}

        return True

    def remove_from_cart(self, user_id, product_name, quantity):
        # Validate user exists and product is in cart with sufficient quantity
        if user_id not in self.users or product_name not in self.users[user_id]['cart']:
            return False

        if quantity <= 0 or self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] < quantity:
            return False

        # Check if product exists in stock
        product_exists = product_name in self.products

        # Return quantity to stock if product exists
        if product_exists:
            self.products[product_name]['stock'] += quantity

        # Update user's cart
        if self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] == quantity:
            del self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name]
        else:
            self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] -= quantity

        # Update global reservations
        if user_id in self.reservations and product_name in self.reservations[user_id]:
            if self.reservations[user_id][product_name] == quantity:
                del self.reservations[user_id][product_name]
                if not self.reservations[user_id]:  # Clean up empty reservations entry
                    del self.reservations[user_id]
            else:
                self.reservations[user_id][product_name] -= quantity

        # Update user's reservations to match global reservations
        if user_id in self.reservations and product_name in self.reservations[user_id]:
            if 'reservations' not in self.users[user_id]:
                self.users[user_id]['reservations'] = {}
            self.users[user_id]['reservations'][product_name] = self.reservations[user_id][product_name]
        else:
            if 'reservations' in self.users[user_id] and product_name in self.users[user_id]['reservations']:
                del self.users[user_id]['reservations'][product_name]
                if not self.users[user_id]['reservations']:
                    del self.users[user_id]['reservations']

        return True

    def checkout(self, user_id):
        # Step 1: Validate user exists and has non-empty cart
        if user_id not in self.users or not self.users[user_id]['cart']:
            return None

        cart = self.users[user_id]['cart']
        price_snapshot = {}
        total_price = 0.0

        # Step 2: Validate all reserved products still exist in the stock system
        for product_name in cart.keys():
            if product_name not in self.products:
                return None

        # Step 3: Validate all reserved products have sufficient stock
        for product_name, quantity in cart.items():
            if self.products[product_name]['stock'] < quantity:
                return None

            # Step 4: Calculate total price using current prices
            price_snapshot[product_name] = self.products[product_name]['price']
            total_price += self.products[product_name]['price'] * quantity

        # Step 5: Generate unique sequential order ID
        order_id = f"SK{self.next_order_id:05d}"

        # Step 6: Create order with status 'pending'
        order_data = {
            'status': 'pending',
            'items': cart.copy(),
            'price_snapshot': price_snapshot,
            'total_price': total_price
        }

        # Step 7: Clear user's cart and reservations
        self.users[user_id]['cart'].clear()
        if 'reservations' in self.users[user_id]:
            self.users[user_id]['reservations'].clear()
        if user_id in self.reservations:
            self.reservations[user_id].clear()

        # Step 8: Add order to orders registry and increment order ID
        self.orders[order_id] = order_data
        self.next_order_id += 1

        return order_id

    def update_order_status(self, order_id, new_status):
        # Validate order exists
        if order_id not in self.orders:
            return False

        current_status = self.orders[order_id]['status']

        # Check if order is already in terminal state
        if current_status == 'cancelled':
            return False

        # Validate status transition
        valid_transitions = {
            'pending': ['processing', 'cancelled'],
            'processing': ['completed', 'cancelled'],
            'completed': ['shipped', 'cancelled'],
            'shipped': ['delivered', 'cancelled'],
            'delivered': ['cancelled']
        }

        # Check if the transition is valid
        if new_status not in valid_transitions.get(current_status, []):
            return False

        # Handle cancellation
        if new_status == 'cancelled':
            for product_name, quantity in self.orders[order_id]['items'].items():
                if product_name in self.products:
                    # Restore stock if product exists
                    self.products[product_name]['stock'] += quantity
                else:
                    # Recreate product with price from order snapshot
                    price = self.orders[order_id]['price_snapshot'].get(product_name, 0.0)
                    self.products[product_name] = {
                        'price': price,
                        'stock': quantity
                    }

        # Update order status
        self.orders[order_id]['status'] = new_status
        return True

def main():
    # Create an instance of the e-shop system
    shop = EShopSystem()

    # Demonstrate user management
    print("=== User Management ===")
    shop.create_user("user1")
    shop.create_user("user2")
    print("Created users:", list(shop.users.keys()))

    # Demonstrate product management
    print("\n=== Product Management ===")
    shop.add_product("Laptop", 999.99, 10)
    shop.add_product("Phone", 699.99, 15)
    print("Available products:", list(shop.products.keys()))

    # Demonstrate adding to cart
    print("\n=== Cart Operations ===")
    shop.add_to_cart("user1", "Laptop", 2)
    shop.add_to_cart("user1", "Phone", 1)
    print("User1's cart:", shop.users["user1"]["cart"])

    # Demonstrate checkout
    print("\n=== Checkout ===")
    order_id = shop.checkout("user1")
    print(f"Order created with ID: {order_id}")
    print("User1's cart after checkout:", shop.users["user1"]["cart"])

    # Demonstrate order status update
    print("\n=== Order Status Update ===")
    shop.update_order_status(order_id, "processing")
    print(f"Order {order_id} status:", shop.orders[order_id]["status"])

    # Demonstrate system state
    print("\n=== System State ===")
    state = shop.get_system_state()
    print("Users:", state["users"])
    print("Products:", state["products"])
    print("Orders:", state["orders"])

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

===== USE_CASE_EXTRACTION_FAILED OUTPUT =====
Here's the structured Use Case derived from the provided program:

---

**Use Case: Manage E-Shop Operations**

**Primary Actor:** Customer, Administrator

**Goal:**
- Customer: Purchase products, manage shopping cart, and track orders
- Administrator: Manage product inventory, monitor system state, and process orders

---

**System Responsibilities:**
1. **User Management**
   - Register new users in the system
   - Remove existing users and release their reserved resources
   - Maintain user-specific shopping carts and reservations

2. **Product Management**
   - Add new products or update existing product stock and prices
   - Remove products from inventory
   - Track product stock levels and prevent overselling

3. **Shopping Cart Operations**
   - Allow users to add/remove products to/from their cart
   - Reserve product stock when items are added to cart
   - Release reserved stock when items are removed from cart or orders are cancelled

4. **Order Processing**
   - Convert user cart into an order with a unique ID
   - Track order status through predefined workflow (pending → processing → completed → shipped → delivered)
   - Support order cancellation and stock restoration
   - Maintain price snapshots at time of order creation

5. **System Monitoring**
   - Provide read-only access to current system state (users, products, orders, reservations)
   - Generate sequential order IDs

---

**Preconditions:**
1. System is initialized with empty user, product, and order registries
2. For user-specific operations: User must exist in the system
3. For product-specific operations: Product must exist in inventory (unless adding new product)
4. For cart/order operations: User must have an active cart

---

**Postconditions:**
1. **User Management**
   - New user is registered with empty cart and reservations
   - Deleted user is removed from system; all reservations are released

2. **Product Management**
   - New product is added to inventory with specified price and stock
   - Existing product stock/price is updated
   - Deleted product is removed from inventory

3. **Shopping Cart Operations**
   - Product is added to user's cart; stock is reserved
   - Product is removed from user's cart; stock is released
   - User's cart and reservations reflect current state

4. **Order Processing**
   - Order is created with unique ID, status "pending," and price snapshot
   - User's cart and reservations are cleared
   - Order status is updated according to valid transitions
   - Cancelled orders restore product stock

5. **System Monitoring**
   - Current system state is returned as a read-only snapshot

---

**Main Success Scenario:**

1. **Register New User**
   - *Actor:* Administrator
   - *System:* Validates user ID is unique and non-empty
   - *System:* Creates new user with empty cart and reservations

2. **Add Product to Inventory**
   - *Actor:* Administrator
   - *System:* Validates product name is non-empty, price and stock are non-negative
   - *System:* Adds new product or updates existing product stock/price

3. **Add Product to Cart**
   - *Actor:* Customer
   - *System:* Validates user exists, product exists, and sufficient stock is available
   - *System:* Adds product to user's cart and reserves stock

4. **Remove Product from Cart**
   - *Actor:* Customer
   - *System:* Validates user exists, product is in cart, and quantity is valid
   - *System:* Removes product from cart and releases stock

5. **Checkout**
   - *Actor:* Customer
   - *System:* Validates user exists, cart is non-empty, and all products are available
   - *System:* Creates order with unique ID, status "pending," and price snapshot
   - *System:* Clears user's cart and reservations

6. **Update Order Status**
   - *Actor:* Administrator
   - *System:* Validates order exists and status transition is valid
   - *System:* Updates order status; restores stock if order is cancelled

7. **View System State**
   - *Actor:* Administrator
   - *System:* Returns read-only snapshot of current system state (users, products, orders, reservations)

---

**Extensions:**

1a. **User Registration Fails**
   - *Condition:* User ID is empty or already exists
   - *System:* Rejects registration and returns failure

2a. **Product Addition Fails**
   - *Condition:* Product name is empty, or price/stock is negative
   - *System:* Rejects addition/update and returns failure

3a. **Add to Cart Fails**
   - *Condition:* User does not exist, product does not exist, or insufficient stock
   - *System:* Rejects addition and returns failure

4a. **Remove from Cart Fails**
   - *Condition:* User does not exist, product not in cart, or invalid quantity
   - *System:* Rejects removal and returns failure

5a. **Checkout Fails**
   - *Condition:* User does not exist, cart is empty, or product is unavailable
   - *System:* Rejects checkout and returns failure

6a. **Order Status Update Fails**
   - *Condition:* Order does not exist, status transition is invalid, or order is already cancelled
   - *System:* Rejects update and returns failure

7a. **View System State**
   - *Condition:* None (always succeeds)
   - *System:* Returns current state snapshot

---

**Special Requirements:**
1. **Stock Reservation**
   - System must prevent overselling by reserving stock when products are added to cart
   - Reserved stock must be released when products are removed from cart or orders are cancelled

2. **Order ID Generation**
   - Order IDs must be unique and sequential (format: SK00001, SK00002, etc.)

3. **Price Snapshot**
   - Order total must be calculated using product prices at time of checkout

4. **Read-Only State Access**
   - System state snapshot must be immutable to prevent external modifications

5. **Status Transitions**
   - Order status must follow predefined workflow (pending → processing → completed → shipped → delivered)
   - Cancellation must be allowed from any non-terminal state

---

===== assemble_program OUTPUT =====
class EShopSystem:
    def __init__(self):
        self.users = {}
        self.products = {}
        self.orders = {}
        self.next_order_id = 1
        self.reservations = {}

    def create_user(self, user_id):
        if not user_id or user_id in self.users:
            return False

        self.users[user_id] = {
            'cart': {},
            'reservations': {}
        }
        return True

    def add_product(self, product_name, price, stock):
        if not product_name or price < 0 or stock < 0:
            return False

        if product_name in self.products:
            self.products[product_name]['stock'] += stock
            self.products[product_name]['price'] = price
        else:
            self.products[product_name] = {
                'price': price,
                'stock': stock
            }

        return True

    def delete_product(self, product_name):
        if product_name not in self.products:
            return False

        del self.products[product_name]
        return True

    def get_system_state(self):
        """Retrieves the current system state, including users, products, stock, carts, and orders.

        Returns:
            dict: A read-only snapshot of the current system state containing:
                - users: Dictionary of user data including carts and reservations
                - products: Dictionary of product data including price and stock
                - orders: Dictionary of order data including status, items, and price snapshot
                - next_order_id: Current next order ID counter
                - reservations: Dictionary of product reservations by user
        """
        from copy import deepcopy
        from types import MappingProxyType

        state = {
            'users': deepcopy(self.users),
            'products': deepcopy(self.products),
            'orders': deepcopy(self.orders),
            'next_order_id': self.next_order_id,
            'reservations': deepcopy(self.reservations)
        }
        return MappingProxyType(state)

    def delete_user(self, user_id):
        if user_id not in self.users:
            return False

        # Process reservations
        reservations = self.users[user_id].get('reservations', {})
        for product_name, quantity in reservations.items():
            if product_name in self.products:
                self.products[product_name]['stock'] += quantity

        # Remove user from reservations tracking
        if user_id in self.reservations:
            del self.reservations[user_id]

        # Remove user
        del self.users[user_id]

        return True

    def add_to_cart(self, user_id, product_name, quantity):
        # Validate inputs
        if quantity <= 0:
            return False

        # Check if user exists
        if user_id not in self.users:
            return False

        # Check if product exists
        if product_name not in self.products:
            return False

        product = self.products[product_name]
        available_stock = product['stock']

        # Calculate total reserved quantity for this product across all users
        total_reserved = 0
        for user_reservations in self.reservations.values():
            if product_name in user_reservations:
                total_reserved += user_reservations[product_name]

        # Calculate total quantity including existing cart items
        if product_name in self.users[user_id]['cart']:
            current_quantity = self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name]
            total_quantity = current_quantity + quantity
        else:
            total_quantity = quantity

        # Check if sufficient stock is available (considering all reservations)
        if total_quantity > (available_stock - total_reserved):
            return False

        # Update user's cart
        if product_name in self.users[user_id]['cart']:
            self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] += quantity
        else:
            self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] = quantity

        # Deduct from available stock
        self.products[product_name]['stock'] -= quantity

        # Update user's reservation
        if user_id in self.reservations:
            if product_name in self.reservations[user_id]:
                self.reservations[user_id][product_name] += quantity
            else:
                self.reservations[user_id][product_name] = quantity
        else:
            self.reservations[user_id] = {product_name: quantity}

        # Update user's reservations to match global reservations
        if 'reservations' not in self.users[user_id]:
            self.users[user_id]['reservations'] = {}
        self.users[user_id]['reservations'][product_name] = self.reservations[user_id][product_name]

        return True

    def remove_from_cart(self, user_id, product_name, quantity):
        # Validate user exists and product is in cart with sufficient quantity
        if user_id not in self.users or product_name not in self.users[user_id]['cart']:
            return False

        if quantity <= 0 or self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] < quantity:
            return False

        # Check if product exists in stock
        product_exists = product_name in self.products

        # Return quantity to stock if product exists
        if product_exists:
            self.products[product_name]['stock'] += quantity

        # Update user's cart
        if self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] == quantity:
            del self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name]
        else:
            self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] -= quantity

        # Update global reservations
        if user_id in self.reservations and product_name in self.reservations[user_id]:
            if self.reservations[user_id][product_name] == quantity:
                del self.reservations[user_id][product_name]
                if not self.reservations[user_id]:  # Clean up empty reservations entry
                    del self.reservations[user_id]
            else:
                self.reservations[user_id][product_name] -= quantity

        # Update user's reservations to match global reservations
        if user_id in self.reservations and product_name in self.reservations[user_id]:
            if 'reservations' not in self.users[user_id]:
                self.users[user_id]['reservations'] = {}
            self.users[user_id]['reservations'][product_name] = self.reservations[user_id][product_name]
        else:
            if 'reservations' in self.users[user_id] and product_name in self.users[user_id]['reservations']:
                del self.users[user_id]['reservations'][product_name]
                if not self.users[user_id]['reservations']:
                    del self.users[user_id]['reservations']

        return True

    def checkout(self, user_id):
        # Step 1: Validate user exists and has non-empty cart
        if user_id not in self.users or not self.users[user_id]['cart']:
            return None

        cart = self.users[user_id]['cart']
        price_snapshot = {}
        total_price = 0.0

        # Step 2: Validate all reserved products still exist in the stock system
        for product_name in cart.keys():
            if product_name not in self.products:
                return None

        # Step 3: Validate all reserved products have sufficient stock
        for product_name, quantity in cart.items():
            if self.products[product_name]['stock'] < quantity:
                return None

            # Step 4: Calculate total price using current prices
            price_snapshot[product_name] = self.products[product_name]['price']
            total_price += self.products[product_name]['price'] * quantity

        # Step 5: Generate unique sequential order ID
        order_id = f"SK{self.next_order_id:05d}"

        # Step 6: Create order with status 'pending'
        order_data = {
            'status': 'pending',
            'items': cart.copy(),
            'price_snapshot': price_snapshot,
            'total_price': total_price
        }

        # Step 7: Clear user's cart and reservations
        self.users[user_id]['cart'].clear()
        if 'reservations' in self.users[user_id]:
            self.users[user_id]['reservations'].clear()
        if user_id in self.reservations:
            self.reservations[user_id].clear()

        # Step 8: Add order to orders registry and increment order ID
        self.orders[order_id] = order_data
        self.next_order_id += 1

        return order_id

    def update_order_status(self, order_id, new_status):
        # Validate order exists
        if order_id not in self.orders:
            return False

        current_status = self.orders[order_id]['status']

        # Check if order is already in terminal state
        if current_status == 'cancelled':
            return False

        # Validate status transition
        valid_transitions = {
            'pending': ['processing', 'cancelled'],
            'processing': ['completed', 'cancelled'],
            'completed': ['shipped', 'cancelled'],
            'shipped': ['delivered', 'cancelled'],
            'delivered': ['cancelled']
        }

        # Check if the transition is valid
        if new_status not in valid_transitions.get(current_status, []):
            return False

        # Handle cancellation
        if new_status == 'cancelled':
            for product_name, quantity in self.orders[order_id]['items'].items():
                if product_name in self.products:
                    # Restore stock if product exists
                    self.products[product_name]['stock'] += quantity
                else:
                    # Recreate product with price from order snapshot
                    price = self.orders[order_id]['price_snapshot'].get(product_name, 0.0)
                    self.products[product_name] = {
                        'price': price,
                        'stock': quantity
                    }

        # Update order status
        self.orders[order_id]['status'] = new_status
        return True

def main():
    # Create an instance of the e-shop system
    shop = EShopSystem()

    # Demonstrate user management
    print("=== User Management ===")
    shop.create_user("user1")
    shop.create_user("user2")
    print("Created users:", list(shop.users.keys()))

    # Demonstrate product management
    print("\n=== Product Management ===")
    shop.add_product("Laptop", 999.99, 10)
    shop.add_product("Phone", 699.99, 15)
    print("Available products:", list(shop.products.keys()))

    # Demonstrate adding to cart
    print("\n=== Cart Operations ===")
    shop.add_to_cart("user1", "Laptop", 2)
    shop.add_to_cart("user1", "Phone", 1)
    print("User1's cart:", shop.users["user1"]["cart"])

    # Demonstrate checkout
    print("\n=== Checkout ===")
    order_id = shop.checkout("user1")
    print(f"Order created with ID: {order_id}")
    print("User1's cart after checkout:", shop.users["user1"]["cart"])

    # Demonstrate order status update
    print("\n=== Order Status Update ===")
    shop.update_order_status(order_id, "processing")
    print(f"Order {order_id} status:", shop.orders[order_id]["status"])

    # Demonstrate system state
    print("\n=== System State ===")
    state = shop.get_system_state()
    print("Users:", state["users"])
    print("Products:", state["products"])
    print("Orders:", state["orders"])

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

===== USE_CASE_EXTRACTION_FAILED OUTPUT =====
Here is the structured Use Case derived from the provided program:

---

**Use Case: Manage E-Shop Operations**

**Primary Actor:** Customer, Administrator

**Goal:**
- Customer: Purchase products, manage shopping cart, and track orders
- Administrator: Manage product inventory, monitor system state, and update order statuses

---

**System Responsibilities:**
1. **User Management**
   - Register new users in the system
   - Remove existing users and release their reserved resources
   - Maintain user-specific shopping carts and reservations

2. **Product Management**
   - Add new products or update existing product stock and prices
   - Remove products from the inventory
   - Track product stock levels and prevent overselling

3. **Shopping Cart Management**
   - Allow users to add products to their cart while reserving stock
   - Enable users to remove products from their cart and release reserved stock
   - Validate product availability before cart modifications

4. **Order Processing**
   - Process user checkouts to create orders with unique identifiers
   - Maintain order status and support valid status transitions
   - Handle order cancellations and restock products

5. **System Monitoring**
   - Provide a read-only snapshot of the current system state (users, products, orders, reservations)

---

**Preconditions:**
1. The e-shop system is initialized and operational.
2. For user-specific actions, the user must exist in the system.
3. For product-specific actions, the product must exist in the inventory (unless adding a new product).
4. For order-specific actions, the order must exist in the system.

---

**Postconditions:**
1. **User Management**
   - New users are registered with an empty cart and no reservations.
   - Deleted users are removed, and their reserved stock is released.

2. **Product Management**
   - New products are added with specified price and stock.
   - Existing products have updated stock and price.
   - Deleted products are removed from the inventory.

3. **Shopping Cart Management**
   - Products added to the cart are reserved, and stock is deducted.
   - Products removed from the cart release reserved stock.

4. **Order Processing**
   - Successful checkout creates an order with a unique ID, clears the user’s cart, and releases reservations.
   - Order status updates follow valid transitions and handle cancellations by restocking products.

5. **System Monitoring**
   - A consistent snapshot of the system state is provided without exposing internal data structures.

---

**Main Success Scenario:**

1. **Register User**
   - *Actor:* Administrator
   - *System:* Validates the user ID is unique and not empty, then registers the user with an empty cart and no reservations.

2. **Add Product to Inventory**
   - *Actor:* Administrator
   - *System:* Validates the product details (name, price, stock), then adds the product or updates existing stock and price.

3. **Add Product to Cart**
   - *Actor:* Customer
   - *System:*
     - Validates the user, product, and quantity.
     - Checks available stock (excluding other users' reservations).
     - Reserves the product by deducting stock and updates the user’s cart and reservations.

4. **Remove Product from Cart**
   - *Actor:* Customer
   - *System:*
     - Validates the user, product, and quantity.
     - Releases the reserved stock and updates the user’s cart and reservations.

5. **Checkout**
   - *Actor:* Customer
   - *System:*
     - Validates the user has a non-empty cart and all products are available.
     - Calculates the total price using current product prices.
     - Generates a unique order ID and creates a "pending" order.
     - Clears the user’s cart and reservations.

6. **Update Order Status**
   - *Actor:* Administrator
   - *System:*
     - Validates the order exists and the status transition is valid.
     - Updates the order status and handles cancellations by restocking products.

7. **View System State**
   - *Actor:* Administrator
   - *System:* Provides a read-only snapshot of users, products, orders, and reservations.

---

**Extensions:**

1. **Register User**
   - *1a. User ID is invalid or already exists*
     - *System:* Rejects the request and notifies the actor.

2. **Add Product to Inventory**
   - *2a. Product details are invalid (e.g., negative price/stock)*
     - *System:* Rejects the request and notifies the actor.
   - *2b. Product already exists*
     - *System:* Updates the existing product’s stock and price.

3. **Add Product to Cart**
   - *3a. User or product does not exist*
     - *System:* Rejects the request and notifies the actor.
   - *3b. Insufficient stock or invalid quantity*
     - *System:* Rejects the request and notifies the actor.

4. **Remove Product from Cart**
   - *4a. User, product, or quantity is invalid*
     - *System:* Rejects the request and notifies the actor.

5. **Checkout**
   - *5a. User has no items in cart or products are unavailable*
     - *System:* Rejects the request and notifies the actor.

6. **Update Order Status**
   - *6a. Order does not exist or transition is invalid*
     - *System:* Rejects the request and notifies the actor.
   - *6b. Order is already cancelled*
     - *System:* Rejects further status updates.

---

**Special Requirements:**
1. **Stock Reservation**
   - The system must ensure stock is reserved when added to a cart and released when removed or upon checkout.
   - Stock reservations must account for all users' carts to prevent overselling.

2. **Order ID Generation**
   - Order IDs must be unique and sequential (e.g., "SK00001").

3. **Data Consistency**
   - The system must maintain consistency between carts, reservations, and product stock at all times.

4. **Read-Only System State**
   - The system state snapshot must be immutable to prevent external modifications.

---

===== assemble_program OUTPUT =====
class EShopSystem:
    def __init__(self):
        self.users = {}
        self.products = {}
        self.orders = {}
        self.next_order_id = 1
        self.reservations = {}

    def create_user(self, user_id):
        if not user_id or user_id in self.users:
            return False

        self.users[user_id] = {
            'cart': {},
            'reservations': {}
        }
        return True

    def add_product(self, product_name, price, stock):
        if not product_name or price < 0 or stock < 0:
            return False

        if product_name in self.products:
            self.products[product_name]['stock'] += stock
            self.products[product_name]['price'] = price
        else:
            self.products[product_name] = {
                'price': price,
                'stock': stock
            }

        return True

    def delete_product(self, product_name):
        if product_name not in self.products:
            return False

        del self.products[product_name]
        return True

    def get_system_state(self):
        """Retrieves the current system state, including users, products, stock, carts, and orders.

        Returns:
            dict: A read-only snapshot of the current system state containing:
                - users: Dictionary of user data including carts and reservations
                - products: Dictionary of product data including price and stock
                - orders: Dictionary of order data including status, items, and price snapshot
                - next_order_id: Current next order ID counter
                - reservations: Dictionary of product reservations by user
        """
        from copy import deepcopy
        from types import MappingProxyType

        state = {
            'users': deepcopy(self.users),
            'products': deepcopy(self.products),
            'orders': deepcopy(self.orders),
            'next_order_id': self.next_order_id,
            'reservations': deepcopy(self.reservations)
        }
        return MappingProxyType(state)

    def delete_user(self, user_id):
        if user_id not in self.users:
            return False

        # Process reservations
        reservations = self.users[user_id].get('reservations', {})
        for product_name, quantity in reservations.items():
            if product_name in self.products:
                self.products[product_name]['stock'] += quantity

        # Remove user from reservations tracking
        if user_id in self.reservations:
            del self.reservations[user_id]

        # Remove user
        del self.users[user_id]

        return True

    def add_to_cart(self, user_id, product_name, quantity):
        # Validate inputs
        if quantity <= 0:
            return False

        # Check if user exists
        if user_id not in self.users:
            return False

        # Check if product exists
        if product_name not in self.products:
            return False

        product = self.products[product_name]
        available_stock = product['stock']

        # Calculate total reserved quantity for this product across all users
        total_reserved = 0
        for user_reservations in self.reservations.values():
            if product_name in user_reservations:
                total_reserved += user_reservations[product_name]

        # Calculate total quantity including existing cart items
        if product_name in self.users[user_id]['cart']:
            current_quantity = self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name]
            total_quantity = current_quantity + quantity
        else:
            total_quantity = quantity

        # Check if sufficient stock is available (considering all reservations)
        if total_quantity > (available_stock - total_reserved):
            return False

        # Update user's cart
        if product_name in self.users[user_id]['cart']:
            self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] += quantity
        else:
            self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] = quantity

        # Deduct from available stock
        self.products[product_name]['stock'] -= quantity

        # Update user's reservation
        if user_id in self.reservations:
            if product_name in self.reservations[user_id]:
                self.reservations[user_id][product_name] += quantity
            else:
                self.reservations[user_id][product_name] = quantity
        else:
            self.reservations[user_id] = {product_name: quantity}

        # Update user's reservations to match global reservations
        if 'reservations' not in self.users[user_id]:
            self.users[user_id]['reservations'] = {}
        self.users[user_id]['reservations'][product_name] = self.reservations[user_id][product_name]

        return True

    def remove_from_cart(self, user_id, product_name, quantity):
        # Validate user exists and product is in cart with sufficient quantity
        if user_id not in self.users or product_name not in self.users[user_id]['cart']:
            return False

        if quantity <= 0 or self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] < quantity:
            return False

        # Check if product exists in stock
        product_exists = product_name in self.products

        # Return quantity to stock if product exists
        if product_exists:
            self.products[product_name]['stock'] += quantity

        # Update user's cart
        if self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] == quantity:
            del self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name]
        else:
            self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] -= quantity

        # Update global reservations
        if user_id in self.reservations and product_name in self.reservations[user_id]:
            if self.reservations[user_id][product_name] == quantity:
                del self.reservations[user_id][product_name]
                if not self.reservations[user_id]:  # Clean up empty reservations entry
                    del self.reservations[user_id]
            else:
                self.reservations[user_id][product_name] -= quantity

        # Update user's reservations to match global reservations
        if user_id in self.reservations and product_name in self.reservations[user_id]:
            if 'reservations' not in self.users[user_id]:
                self.users[user_id]['reservations'] = {}
            self.users[user_id]['reservations'][product_name] = self.reservations[user_id][product_name]
        else:
            if 'reservations' in self.users[user_id] and product_name in self.users[user_id]['reservations']:
                del self.users[user_id]['reservations'][product_name]
                if not self.users[user_id]['reservations']:
                    del self.users[user_id]['reservations']

        return True

    def checkout(self, user_id):
        # Step 1: Validate user exists and has non-empty cart
        if user_id not in self.users or not self.users[user_id]['cart']:
            return None

        cart = self.users[user_id]['cart']
        price_snapshot = {}
        total_price = 0.0

        # Step 2: Validate all reserved products still exist in the stock system
        for product_name in cart.keys():
            if product_name not in self.products:
                return None

        # Step 3: Validate all reserved products have sufficient stock
        for product_name, quantity in cart.items():
            if self.products[product_name]['stock'] < quantity:
                return None

            # Step 4: Calculate total price using current prices
            price_snapshot[product_name] = self.products[product_name]['price']
            total_price += self.products[product_name]['price'] * quantity

        # Step 5: Generate unique sequential order ID
        order_id = f"SK{self.next_order_id:05d}"

        # Step 6: Create order with status 'pending'
        order_data = {
            'status': 'pending',
            'items': cart.copy(),
            'price_snapshot': price_snapshot,
            'total_price': total_price
        }

        # Step 7: Clear user's cart and reservations
        self.users[user_id]['cart'].clear()
        if 'reservations' in self.users[user_id]:
            self.users[user_id]['reservations'].clear()
        if user_id in self.reservations:
            self.reservations[user_id].clear()

        # Step 8: Add order to orders registry and increment order ID
        self.orders[order_id] = order_data
        self.next_order_id += 1

        return order_id

    def update_order_status(self, order_id, new_status):
        # Validate order exists
        if order_id not in self.orders:
            return False

        current_status = self.orders[order_id]['status']

        # Check if order is already in terminal state
        if current_status in ['cancelled', 'delivered']:
            return False

        # Validate status transition
        valid_transitions = {
            'pending': ['processing', 'cancelled'],
            'processing': ['completed', 'cancelled'],
            'completed': ['shipped', 'cancelled'],
            'shipped': ['delivered', 'cancelled'],
            'delivered': ['cancelled']
        }

        # Check if the transition is valid
        if new_status not in valid_transitions.get(current_status, []):
            return False

        # Handle cancellation
        if new_status == 'cancelled':
            for product_name, quantity in self.orders[order_id]['items'].items():
                if product_name in self.products:
                    # Restore stock if product exists
                    self.products[product_name]['stock'] += quantity
                else:
                    # Recreate product with price from order snapshot
                    price = self.orders[order_id]['price_snapshot'].get(product_name, 0.0)
                    if price >= 0:  # Ensure price is valid
                        self.products[product_name] = {
                            'price': price,
                            'stock': quantity
                        }

        # Update order status
        self.orders[order_id]['status'] = new_status
        return True

def main():
    # Create an instance of the e-shop system
    shop = EShopSystem()

    # Demonstrate user management
    print("=== User Management ===")
    shop.create_user("user1")
    shop.create_user("user2")
    print("Created users:", list(shop.users.keys()))

    # Demonstrate product management
    print("\n=== Product Management ===")
    shop.add_product("Laptop", 999.99, 10)
    shop.add_product("Phone", 699.99, 15)
    print("Available products:", list(shop.products.keys()))

    # Demonstrate adding to cart
    print("\n=== Cart Operations ===")
    shop.add_to_cart("user1", "Laptop", 2)
    shop.add_to_cart("user1", "Phone", 1)
    print("User1's cart:", shop.users["user1"]["cart"])

    # Demonstrate checkout
    print("\n=== Checkout ===")
    order_id = shop.checkout("user1")
    print(f"Order created with ID: {order_id}")
    print("User1's cart after checkout:", shop.users["user1"]["cart"])

    # Demonstrate order status update
    print("\n=== Order Status Update ===")
    shop.update_order_status(order_id, "processing")
    print(f"Order {order_id} status:", shop.orders[order_id]["status"])

    # Demonstrate system state
    print("\n=== System State ===")
    state = shop.get_system_state()
    print("Users:", state["users"])
    print("Products:", state["products"])
    print("Orders:", state["orders"])

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

===== USE_CASE_EXTRACTION_FAILED OUTPUT =====
Here's the structured Use Case derived from the given program:

---

**Use Case: Manage E-Shop Operations**

**Primary Actor:** Customer, Administrator

**Goal:**
- Customer: Purchase products through the e-shop system by browsing, adding items to cart, and completing checkout.
- Administrator: Manage product inventory, user accounts, and order processing within the e-shop system.

---

**System Responsibilities:**
1. **User Management**
   - Create new user accounts with unique identifiers.
   - Delete existing user accounts and release associated resources (e.g., reservations, cart items).
   - Maintain user-specific data, including shopping carts and product reservations.

2. **Product Management**
   - Add new products to the inventory with specified price and stock quantity.
   - Update existing product details (price, stock) when re-adding products.
   - Remove products from the inventory.
   - Track and manage product stock levels, including reserved quantities.

3. **Shopping Cart Operations**
   - Allow users to add products to their shopping cart, reserving stock for the selected quantity.
   - Enable users to remove products from their shopping cart, releasing reserved stock.
   - Validate product availability and stock sufficiency before cart modifications.

4. **Checkout Process**
   - Convert a user’s shopping cart into an order with a unique identifier.
   - Calculate the total order price using current product prices.
   - Clear the user’s cart and reservations upon successful checkout.
   - Generate a snapshot of product prices at the time of checkout for order consistency.

5. **Order Management**
   - Track orders with unique sequential identifiers.
   - Update order status through predefined transitions (e.g., pending → processing → completed → shipped → delivered or cancelled).
   - Handle order cancellations by restoring product stock or recreating products if necessary.
   - Prevent invalid status transitions (e.g., delivered → processing).

6. **System State Monitoring**
   - Provide a read-only snapshot of the current system state, including users, products, orders, and reservations.

---

**Preconditions:**
1. The e-shop system is operational and accessible.
2. For user-specific actions (e.g., add to cart, checkout), the user must exist in the system.
3. For product-specific actions (e.g., add to cart, checkout), the product must exist in the inventory.
4. For order status updates, the order must exist in the system.

---

**Postconditions:**
1. **User Management**
   - New users are registered in the system with an empty cart and no reservations.
   - Deleted users are removed from the system, and their reservations are released.

2. **Product Management**
   - New products are added to the inventory with the specified price and stock.
   - Existing products have their stock and price updated if re-added.
   - Deleted products are removed from the inventory.

3. **Shopping Cart Operations**
   - Products added to the cart are reserved in the inventory, reducing available stock.
   - Products removed from the cart have their stock released back to the inventory.

4. **Checkout Process**
   - The user’s cart is converted into an order with a unique ID, and the cart is cleared.
   - The order is recorded in the system with a "pending" status and a price snapshot.

5. **Order Management**
   - Order status is updated according to valid transitions.
   - Cancelled orders have their stock restored or products recreated if necessary.

6. **System State Monitoring**
   - A snapshot of the system state is provided without allowing modifications.

---

**Main Success Scenario:**

1. **User Registration**
   - *Actor:* Administrator
   - *System:* Validates the user ID is unique and not empty.
   - *System:* Creates a new user account with an empty cart and no reservations.

2. **Add Product to Inventory**
   - *Actor:* Administrator
   - *System:* Validates the product name is not empty, and price/stock are non-negative.
   - *System:* Adds the product to the inventory or updates stock/price if the product exists.

3. **Add Product to Cart**
   - *Actor:* Customer
   - *System:* Validates the user exists, the product exists, and the requested quantity is positive.
   - *System:* Checks if sufficient stock is available (accounting for other reservations).
   - *System:* Reserves the product quantity, updates the user’s cart, and reduces available stock.

4. **Remove Product from Cart**
   - *Actor:* Customer
   - *System:* Validates the user exists, the product is in the cart, and the quantity is valid.
   - *System:* Releases the reserved stock, updates the user’s cart, and removes the reservation.

5. **Checkout**
   - *Actor:* Customer
   - *System:* Validates the user exists and the cart is not empty.
   - *System:* Validates all products in the cart exist and have sufficient stock.
   - *System:* Calculates the total order price using current product prices.
   - *System:* Generates a unique order ID and creates an order with "pending" status.
   - *System:* Clears the user’s cart and reservations.

6. **Update Order Status**
   - *Actor:* Administrator
   - *System:* Validates the order exists and the current status allows the transition.
   - *System:* Updates the order status to the new value.
   - *System:* If cancelling, restores product stock or recreates products if necessary.

7. **View System State**
   - *Actor:* Administrator
   - *System:* Provides a read-only snapshot of users, products, orders, and reservations.

---

**Extensions:**

1. **User Registration Fails**
   - *1a. User ID is empty or already exists.*
     - *System:* Returns failure; user account is not created.

2. **Add Product to Inventory Fails**
   - *2a. Product name is empty, or price/stock is negative.*
     - *System:* Returns failure; product is not added/updated.
   - *2b. Product already exists.*
     - *System:* Updates the existing product’s stock and price.

3. **Add Product to Cart Fails**
   - *3a. User does not exist.*
     - *System:* Returns failure; cart is not updated.
   - *3b. Product does not exist.*
     - *System:* Returns failure; cart is not updated.
   - *3c. Requested quantity is invalid (≤ 0).*
     - *System:* Returns failure; cart is not updated.
   - *3d. Insufficient stock available (including reservations).*
     - *System:* Returns failure; cart is not updated.

4. **Remove Product from Cart Fails**
   - *4a. User does not exist or product is not in the cart.*
     - *System:* Returns failure; cart is not updated.
   - *4b. Requested quantity is invalid (≤ 0 or exceeds cart quantity).*
     - *System:* Returns failure; cart is not updated.

5. **Checkout Fails**
   - *5a. User does not exist or cart is empty.*
     - *System:* Returns failure; no order is created.
   - *5b. Product in cart no longer exists.*
     - *System:* Returns failure; no order is created.
   - *5c. Product in cart has insufficient stock.*
     - *System:* Returns failure; no order is created.

6. **Update Order Status Fails**
   - *6a. Order does not exist.*
     - *System:* Returns failure; status is not updated.
   - *6b. Order is in a terminal state (cancelled or delivered).*
     - *System:* Returns failure; status is not updated.
   - *6c. Invalid status transition requested.*
     - *System:* Returns failure; status is not updated.

7. **Delete User Fails**
   - *7a. User does not exist.*
     - *System:* Returns failure; user is not deleted.

8. **Delete Product Fails**
   - *8a. Product does not exist.*
     - *System:* Returns failure; product is not deleted.

---

**Special Requirements:**
1. **Stock Reservation:**
   - The system must ensure that product stock is reserved when added to a cart and released when removed or upon checkout.
   - Stock reservations must account for all users to prevent overselling.

2. **Order Consistency:**
   - The system must capture a snapshot of product prices at the time of checkout to ensure order total consistency, even if prices change later.

3. **Order ID Generation:**
   - Order IDs must be unique and sequential, formatted as "SK" followed by a 5-digit number (e.g., SK00001).

4. **Read-Only System State:**
   - The system state snapshot provided by `get_system_state` must be read-only to prevent unintended modifications.

5. **Status Transitions:**
   - Order status transitions must follow a predefined flow (e.g., pending → processing → completed → shipped → delivered or cancelled) and reject invalid transitions.

---

===== assemble_program OUTPUT =====
class EShopSystem:
    def __init__(self):
        self.users = {}
        self.products = {}
        self.orders = {}
        self.next_order_id = 1
        self.reservations = {}

    def create_user(self, user_id):
        if not user_id or user_id in self.users:
            return False

        self.users[user_id] = {
            'cart': {},
            'reservations': {}
        }
        return True

    def add_product(self, product_name, price, stock):
        if not product_name or price < 0 or stock < 0:
            return False

        if product_name in self.products:
            self.products[product_name]['stock'] += stock
            self.products[product_name]['price'] = price
        else:
            self.products[product_name] = {
                'price': price,
                'stock': stock
            }

        return True

    def delete_product(self, product_name):
        if product_name not in self.products:
            return False

        del self.products[product_name]
        return True

    def get_system_state(self):
        """Retrieves the current system state, including users, products, stock, carts, and orders.

        Returns:
            dict: A read-only snapshot of the current system state containing:
                - users: Dictionary of user data including carts and reservations
                - products: Dictionary of product data including price and stock
                - orders: Dictionary of order data including status, items, and price snapshot
                - next_order_id: Current next order ID counter
                - reservations: Dictionary of product reservations by user
        """
        from copy import deepcopy
        from types import MappingProxyType

        state = {
            'users': deepcopy(self.users),
            'products': deepcopy(self.products),
            'orders': deepcopy(self.orders),
            'next_order_id': self.next_order_id,
            'reservations': deepcopy(self.reservations)
        }
        return MappingProxyType(state)

    def delete_user(self, user_id):
        if user_id not in self.users:
            return False

        # Process reservations
        reservations = self.users[user_id].get('reservations', {})
        for product_name, quantity in reservations.items():
            if product_name in self.products:
                self.products[product_name]['stock'] += quantity

        # Remove user from reservations tracking
        if user_id in self.reservations:
            del self.reservations[user_id]

        # Remove user
        del self.users[user_id]

        return True

    def add_to_cart(self, user_id, product_name, quantity):
        # Validate inputs
        if quantity <= 0:
            return False

        # Check if user exists
        if user_id not in self.users:
            return False

        # Check if product exists
        if product_name not in self.products:
            return False

        product = self.products[product_name]
        available_stock = product['stock']

        # Calculate total reserved quantity for this product across all users
        total_reserved = 0
        for user_reservations in self.reservations.values():
            if product_name in user_reservations:
                total_reserved += user_reservations[product_name]

        # Calculate total quantity including existing cart items
        if product_name in self.users[user_id]['cart']:
            current_quantity = self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name]
            total_quantity = current_quantity + quantity
        else:
            total_quantity = quantity

        # Check if sufficient stock is available (considering all reservations)
        if total_quantity > (available_stock - total_reserved):
            return False

        # Update user's cart
        if product_name in self.users[user_id]['cart']:
            self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] += quantity
        else:
            self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] = quantity

        # Deduct from available stock
        self.products[product_name]['stock'] -= quantity

        # Update user's reservation
        if user_id in self.reservations:
            if product_name in self.reservations[user_id]:
                self.reservations[user_id][product_name] += quantity
            else:
                self.reservations[user_id][product_name] = quantity
        else:
            self.reservations[user_id] = {product_name: quantity}

        # Update user's reservations to match global reservations
        if 'reservations' not in self.users[user_id]:
            self.users[user_id]['reservations'] = {}
        self.users[user_id]['reservations'][product_name] = self.reservations[user_id][product_name]

        return True

    def remove_from_cart(self, user_id, product_name, quantity):
        # Validate user exists and product is in cart with sufficient quantity
        if user_id not in self.users or product_name not in self.users[user_id]['cart']:
            return False

        if quantity <= 0 or self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] < quantity:
            return False

        # Check if product exists in stock
        product_exists = product_name in self.products

        # Return quantity to stock if product exists
        if product_exists:
            self.products[product_name]['stock'] += quantity

        # Update user's cart
        if self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] == quantity:
            del self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name]
        else:
            self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] -= quantity

        # Update global reservations
        if user_id in self.reservations and product_name in self.reservations[user_id]:
            if self.reservations[user_id][product_name] == quantity:
                del self.reservations[user_id][product_name]
                if not self.reservations[user_id]:  # Clean up empty reservations entry
                    del self.reservations[user_id]
            else:
                self.reservations[user_id][product_name] -= quantity

        # Update user's reservations to match global reservations
        if user_id in self.reservations and product_name in self.reservations[user_id]:
            if 'reservations' not in self.users[user_id]:
                self.users[user_id]['reservations'] = {}
            self.users[user_id]['reservations'][product_name] = self.reservations[user_id][product_name]
        else:
            if 'reservations' in self.users[user_id] and product_name in self.users[user_id]['reservations']:
                del self.users[user_id]['reservations'][product_name]
                if not self.users[user_id]['reservations']:
                    del self.users[user_id]['reservations']

        return True

    def checkout(self, user_id):
        # Step 1: Validate user exists and has non-empty cart
        if user_id not in self.users or not self.users[user_id]['cart']:
            return None

        cart = self.users[user_id]['cart']
        price_snapshot = {}
        total_price = 0.0

        # Step 2: Validate all reserved products still exist in the stock system
        for product_name in cart.keys():
            if product_name not in self.products:
                return None

        # Step 3: Validate all reserved products have sufficient stock
        for product_name, quantity in cart.items():
            if self.products[product_name]['stock'] < quantity:
                return None

            # Step 4: Calculate total price using current prices
            price_snapshot[product_name] = self.products[product_name]['price']
            total_price += self.products[product_name]['price'] * quantity

        # Step 5: Generate unique sequential order ID
        order_id = f"SK{self.next_order_id:05d}"

        # Step 6: Create order with status 'pending'
        order_data = {
            'status': 'pending',
            'items': cart.copy(),
            'price_snapshot': price_snapshot,
            'total_price': total_price
        }

        # Step 7: Clear user's cart and reservations
        self.users[user_id]['cart'].clear()
        if 'reservations' in self.users[user_id]:
            self.users[user_id]['reservations'].clear()
        if user_id in self.reservations:
            self.reservations[user_id].clear()

        # Step 8: Add order to orders registry and increment order ID
        self.orders[order_id] = order_data
        self.next_order_id += 1

        return order_id

    def update_order_status(self, order_id, new_status):
        # Validate order exists
        if order_id not in self.orders:
            return False

        current_status = self.orders[order_id]['status']

        # Check if order is already in terminal state
        if current_status in ['cancelled', 'delivered']:
            return False

        # Validate status transition
        valid_transitions = {
            'pending': ['processing', 'cancelled'],
            'processing': ['completed', 'cancelled'],
            'completed': ['shipped', 'cancelled'],
            'shipped': ['delivered', 'cancelled'],
            'delivered': ['cancelled']
        }

        # Check if the transition is valid
        if new_status not in valid_transitions.get(current_status, []):
            return False

        # Handle cancellation
        if new_status == 'cancelled':
            for product_name, quantity in self.orders[order_id]['items'].items():
                if product_name in self.products:
                    # Restore stock if product exists
                    self.products[product_name]['stock'] += quantity
                else:
                    # Recreate product with price from order snapshot
                    price = self.orders[order_id]['price_snapshot'].get(product_name, 0.0)
                    if price >= 0:  # Ensure price is valid
                        self.products[product_name] = {
                            'price': price,
                            'stock': quantity
                        }

        # Update order status
        self.orders[order_id]['status'] = new_status
        return True

def main():
    # Create an instance of the e-shop system
    shop = EShopSystem()

    # Demonstrate user management
    print("=== User Management ===")
    shop.create_user("user1")
    shop.create_user("user2")
    print("Created users:", list(shop.users.keys()))

    # Demonstrate product management
    print("\n=== Product Management ===")
    shop.add_product("Laptop", 999.99, 10)
    shop.add_product("Phone", 699.99, 15)
    print("Available products:", list(shop.products.keys()))

    # Demonstrate adding to cart
    print("\n=== Cart Operations ===")
    shop.add_to_cart("user1", "Laptop", 2)
    shop.add_to_cart("user1", "Phone", 1)
    print("User1's cart:", shop.users["user1"]["cart"])

    # Demonstrate checkout
    print("\n=== Checkout ===")
    order_id = shop.checkout("user1")
    print(f"Order created with ID: {order_id}")
    print("User1's cart after checkout:", shop.users["user1"]["cart"])

    # Demonstrate order status update
    print("\n=== Order Status Update ===")
    shop.update_order_status(order_id, "processing")
    print(f"Order {order_id} status:", shop.orders[order_id]["status"])

    # Demonstrate system state
    print("\n=== System State ===")
    state = shop.get_system_state()
    print("Users:", state["users"])
    print("Products:", state["products"])
    print("Orders:", state["orders"])

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

===== generate_integration_test OUTPUT =====
def test_main_success_scenario():
    system = EShopSystem()

    # Create a user
    assert system.create_user("user1") is True

    # Add a product
    assert system.add_product("laptop", 999.99, 10) is True

    # Add product to cart
    assert system.add_to_cart("user1", "laptop", 2) is True

    # Checkout
    order_id = system.checkout("user1")
    assert order_id is not None

    # Verify order exists
    state = system.get_system_state()
    assert order_id in state["orders"]
    assert state["orders"][order_id]["status"] == "pending"

def test_user_deletion_with_reservations():
    system = EShopSystem()

    # Create user and add product
    assert system.create_user("user2") is True
    assert system.add_product("phone", 599.99, 5) is True

    # Add product to cart and checkout
    assert system.add_to_cart("user2", "phone", 3) is True
    order_id = system.checkout("user2")
    assert order_id is not None

    # Delete user
    assert system.delete_user("user2") is True

    # Verify user and reservations are removed
    state = system.get_system_state()
    assert "user2" not in state["users"]
    assert "user2" not in state["reservations"]

    # Verify stock was updated
    assert state["products"]["phone"]["stock"] == 5  # 5 original + 3 returned

def test_product_deletion_with_active_orders():
    system = EShopSystem()

    # Create user and add product
    assert system.create_user("user3") is True
    assert system.add_product("tablet", 299.99, 8) is True

    # Add product to cart and checkout
    assert system.add_to_cart("user3", "tablet", 4) is True
    order_id = system.checkout("user3")
    assert order_id is not None

    # Delete product
    assert system.delete_product("tablet") is True

    # Verify product is removed from available stock
    state = system.get_system_state()
    assert "tablet" not in state["products"]

    # Verify order still exists
    assert order_id in state["orders"]
    assert state["orders"][order_id]["status"] == "pending"

===== OUTPUT_generate_activity_diagram OUTPUT =====
@startuml
start
:initialize e-shop system;
:create users;
if (user ID valid and not duplicate) then (yes)
  :create user with empty cart;
  :add user to registry;
else (no)
  :reject user creation;
endif

:add products;
if (product data valid) then (yes)
  if (product exists) then (yes)
    :update product stock and price;
  else (no)
    :create new product entry;
  endif
else (no)
  :ignore invalid product;
endif

:add items to cart;
if (user exists and product available) then (yes)
  if (product in cart) then (yes)
    :validate new quantity available;
  endif
  :deduct quantity from stock;
  :update user cart;
  :update user reservation;
else (no)
  :ignore cart addition;
endif

:remove items from cart;
if (user exists and product in cart) then (yes)
  if (product exists in stock) then (yes)
    :return quantity to stock;
  endif
  :update user cart;
  :update user reservation;
else (no)
  :ignore cart removal;
endif

:process checkout;
if (user exists and cart not empty) then (yes)
  :validate all reserved products exist;
  if (all products exist) then (yes)
    :calculate total price;
    :generate sequential order ID;
    :create order with pending status;
    :clear user cart and reservations;
    :add order to registry;
  else (no)
    :cancel checkout;
  endif
else (no)
  :ignore checkout;
endif

:update order status;
if (order exists and valid transition) then (yes)
  if (new status is cancelled) then (yes)
    :restore stock for each product;
    if (product no longer exists) then (yes)
      :recreate product with order price;
    endif
  endif
  :update order status;
else (no)
  :ignore status update;
endif

:retrieve system state;
:return read-only snapshot of users, products, orders;

stop
@enduml
===============================


===== OUTPUT_review_activity_diagram OUTPUT =====
Here are the identified defects in the activity diagram:

- Missing validation for negative/zero quantity in "add items to cart" and "remove items from cart" steps (program checks `quantity <= 0` but diagram omits this)
- Diagram shows ":validate new quantity available;" only when product is in cart, but program always validates total quantity including existing cart items (flow is incomplete)
- "update user reservation" step in "add items to cart" and "remove items from cart" is too abstract - program explicitly synchronizes `self.reservations` and `user['reservations']` (missing key behavior)
- Missing "delete product" and "delete user" flows which exist in the program but are absent in the diagram
- "retrieve system state" step incorrectly shows a single action - program returns a deep copy with multiple components (users, products, orders, reservations, next_order_id)
- Diagram omits the sequential order ID generation logic (`"SK{self.next_order_id:05d}"`) in checkout process (key behavior missing)
- "update order status" flow does not show the terminal state check (`if current_status in ['cancelled', 'delivered']`) that exists in the program
===============================
