===============================


===== user_feedback OUTPUT =====
Your system incorrectly mixes two inventory models:

It decreases stock when adding to cart,
It also tracks reservations and subtracts them during availability checks
This leads to double-counting reserved items and causes:

incorrect cart quantities,
failed checkouts,
inconsistent stock behavior.

Stock should represent physical inventory and must not be changed during cart operations. Update stock only during checkout (reduce) and order cancellation (increase).
===============================


===== OUTPUT_generate_use_case OUTPUT =====
Here’s the revised Use Case with requirement-level changes applied to address the user feedback:

---

### **Use Case: Manage Users in E-Shop Backend**
**Primary Actor:** Administrator
**Goal:** Create or delete users in the system while maintaining consistency of carts, reservations, and stock.

**System Responsibilities:**
- Maintain a registry of users with unique IDs.
- Automatically create an empty cart for each new user.
- Prevent duplicate user creation.
- Handle user deletion by removing their cart, canceling reservations, and updating stock.
- Preserve existing orders of deleted users.

**Preconditions:**
- The system is operational and accessible.

**Postconditions:**
- A new user is created with an empty cart, or an existing user is deleted with their cart and reservations removed.
- Stock is updated if products from canceled reservations still exist.
- **Existing orders of the deleted user remain unaffected in the system.**

**Main Success Scenario (Create User):**
1. Administrator requests to create a user with a unique ID.
2. System validates the ID is not already in use.
3. System creates the user and assigns an empty cart.
4. System confirms successful creation.

**Extensions:**
2a. User ID already exists → System rejects the request and notifies the administrator.
1-4a. Invalid input (e.g., empty ID) → System ignores the request.

**Main Success Scenario (Delete User):**
1. Administrator requests to delete a user by ID.
2. System validates the user exists.
3. System removes the user’s cart and cancels all their reservations.
4. For each canceled reservation:
   - If the product exists in stock, the reserved quantity is returned to available stock.
   - If the product no longer exists, the reservation is removed without stock updates.
5. **System ensures existing orders of the deleted user remain unaffected.**
6. System confirms successful deletion.

**Extensions:**
2a. User does not exist → System ignores the request.
3-6a. System fails to process reservations → No changes are applied (atomicity).

---

### **Use Case: Manage Products in E-Shop Backend**
**Primary Actor:** Administrator
**Goal:** Add or delete products while maintaining stock consistency and preserving existing reservations/orders.

**System Responsibilities:**
- Maintain a product catalog with names, prices, and stock.
- Allow zero stock for products.
- Update existing products if a duplicate name is provided (increase stock, update price).
- Delete products only from available stock, leaving reservations/orders intact.

**Preconditions:**
- The system is operational.

**Postconditions:**
- A new product is added or an existing product is updated/deleted.
- Available stock reflects changes without affecting reservations/orders.
- **Reservations and orders for deleted products remain intact.**

**Main Success Scenario (Add/Update Product):**
1. Administrator requests to add a product with name, price, and stock.
2. System checks if the product name already exists.
3. If the product exists:
   - System increases its stock by the provided quantity.
   - System updates its price to the new value.
4. If the product does not exist:
   - System creates a new product entry.
5. System confirms the operation.

**Extensions:**
1a. Invalid input (e.g., negative price/stock) → System ignores the request.
2-4a. System fails to update stock → No changes are applied.

**Main Success Scenario (Delete Product):**
1. Administrator requests to delete a product by name.
2. System validates the product exists in available stock.
3. System removes the product **only from available stock, leaving reservations and orders unchanged.**
4. **System ensures reservations and orders for the deleted product remain intact.**
5. System confirms deletion.

**Extensions:**
2a. Product does not exist → System ignores the request.
3a. Product has active reservations/orders → System retains them unchanged.

---

### **Use Case: Modify User Cart**
**Primary Actor:** User
**Goal:** Add or remove products from their cart, creating/updating reservations **without affecting stock until checkout**.

**System Responsibilities:**
- Validate product availability before adding to cart **without modifying stock**.
- Track reservations separately from stock.
- Block reserved quantities from other users **during checkout validation**.
- Handle cart modifications atomically.
- **Stock is only updated during checkout (reduce) or order cancellation (increase).**

**Preconditions:**
- The user exists and has an active cart.

**Postconditions:**
- The cart reflects the requested changes.
- Reservations are updated accordingly.
- **Stock remains unchanged until checkout or cancellation.**

**Main Success Scenario (Add Product to Cart):**
1. User requests to add a product with a specified quantity to their cart.
2. System validates the product exists and the quantity is available **in stock (excluding existing reservations)**.
3. System creates/updates a reservation for the product **without modifying stock**.
4. System confirms the addition.

**Extensions:**
2a. Product does not exist or quantity is unavailable → System ignores the request.
2b. Requested quantity is zero → System ignores the request.
3a. Product already in cart → System **validates the new quantity is available (stock - existing reservations), then increases the reserved quantity by the new quantity.**

**Main Success Scenario (Remove Product from Cart):**
1. User requests to remove a product with a specified quantity from their cart.
2. System validates the product exists in the cart and the quantity does not exceed the reserved amount.
3. System updates the reservation **without modifying stock**.
4. System confirms the removal.

**Extensions:**
2a. Product not in cart or quantity exceeds reservation → System ignores the request.

---

### **Use Case: Perform Checkout**
**Primary Actor:** User
**Goal:** Convert a cart into an order with atomic success/failure handling, **updating stock only during this step**.

**System Responsibilities:**
- Validate cart contents and product existence.
- **Validate that reserved quantities are still available in stock (excluding other reservations).**
- Create an order with a unique sequential ID and price snapshot.
- **Reduce stock for all products in the cart.**
- Clear the cart and reservations on success.
- Ensure atomicity (no partial changes on failure).

**Preconditions:**
- The user has a non-empty cart.

**Postconditions:**
- A new order is created with status "pending," or the checkout fails with no changes.
- **Stock is reduced for all products in the cart.**
- **The order ID is globally unique and strictly sequential across the entire system.**

**Main Success Scenario:**
1. User requests to checkout their cart.
2. System validates all reserved products still exist and have sufficient stock **excluding other reservations**.
3. **If any reserved product no longer exists or lacks sufficient stock, the system cancels checkout and notifies the user.**
4. System calculates the total price using current product prices.
5. System **generates a globally unique and strictly sequential order ID (e.g., "SK00001").**
6. System creates an order with status "pending" and stores the price snapshot.
7. **System reduces stock for all products in the cart by their reserved quantities.**
8. System clears the cart and reservations.
9. System confirms the order.

**Extensions:**
4a. Order ID generation fails → System cancels checkout.
7a. System fails to reduce stock or clear cart/reservations → No order is created (atomic rollback).

---

### **Use Case: Update Order Status**
**Primary Actor:** Administrator
**Goal:** Transition an order’s status while enforcing valid sequences and handling cancellations, **restoring stock only during cancellation**.

**System Responsibilities:**
- Enforce valid status transitions (e.g., pending → processing → completed).
- Allow cancellation from any state, including "delivered."
- **Restore stock for canceled orders (recreate products if necessary).**
- **Ignore further status changes if the order is in a terminal state (e.g., "cancelled").**

**Preconditions:**
- The order exists.

**Postconditions:**
- The order’s status is updated, or the request is ignored.
- **If the order is cancelled, stock is restored and the order becomes a terminal state.**

**Main Success Scenario:**
1. Administrator requests to update an order’s status.
2. System validates the transition is allowed (e.g., pending → processing, or any state → cancelled).
3. **If the status is set to "cancelled":**
   - For each product in the order:
     - If the product exists, increase its stock by the ordered quantity.
     - If the product no longer exists, **recreate it with the price stored in the order.**
4. **If the order is already in a terminal state (e.g., "cancelled"), the system ignores the request.**
5. System updates the status.
6. System confirms the update.

**Extensions:**
2a. Invalid transition (e.g., delivered → processing, unless transitioning to "cancelled") → System ignores the request.
3a. Transition to "cancelled":
   - For each product in the order:
     - If the product exists, increase its stock by the ordered quantity.
     - If the product no longer exists, recreate it with the price stored in the order.
4a. Order is already "cancelled" → System ignores further status changes.

---

### **Use Case: Access System State**
**Primary Actor:** Administrator/User
**Goal:** Retrieve current system state (users, products, stock, carts, orders).

**System Responsibilities:**
- Provide read-only access to:
  - Current users and their carts.
  - Products (name, price, available stock **excluding reservations**).
  - Reservations and orders with statuses.

**Preconditions:**
- The system is operational.

**Postconditions:**
- The requested state is returned without modifications.

**Main Success Scenario:**
1. Actor requests system state (e.g., list of users).
2. System **provides read-only access to the data, including reservations and orders with their statuses, and stock availability (stock - reservations).**

**Extensions:**
1a. Invalid request (e.g., non-existent entity) → System returns an empty or error response.

---
===============================

{
  "score": 0.95,
  "issues": [
    "In the 'Modify User Cart' use case, the System Responsibilities section still mentions 'Return stock if products are removed from the cart,' which contradicts the feedback requirement that stock should only be updated during checkout or cancellation.",
    "The 'Main Success Scenario (Remove Product from Cart)' in 'Modify User Cart' use case does not explicitly state that stock remains unchanged, which could lead to misinterpretation despite the correct behavior being implied elsewhere.",
    "The 'Access System State' use case's System Responsibilities section mentions 'stock availability (stock - reservations),' but the feedback implies that stock should represent physical inventory without subtracting reservations. This may cause confusion in the system's state representation.",
    "The 'Perform Checkout' use case's step 7 ('System reduces stock for all products in the cart by their reserved quantities') is correct but could be more explicitly tied to the feedback's requirement that stock updates only occur during checkout or cancellation for clarity."

- Allow cancellation from any state, including "delivered."
- **Restore stock for canceled orders (recreate products if necessary).**
- **Ignore further status changes if the order is in a terminal state (e.g., "cancelled").**

**Preconditions:**
- The order exists.

**Postconditions:**
- The order’s status is updated, or the request is ignored.
- **If the order is cancelled, stock is restored and the order becomes a terminal state.**

**Main Success Scenario:**
1. Administrator requests to update an order’s status.
2. System validates the transition is allowed (e.g., pending → processing, or any state → cancelled).
3. **If the status is set to "cancelled":**
   - For each product in the order:
     - If the product exists, increase its stock by the ordered quantity.
     - If the product no longer exists, **recreate it with the price stored in the order.**
4. **If the order is already in a terminal state (e.g., "cancelled"), the system ignores the request.**
5. System updates the status.
6. System confirms the update.

**Extensions:**
2a. Invalid transition (e.g., delivered → processing, unless transitioning to "cancelled") → System ignores the request.
3a. Transition to "cancelled":
   - For each product in the order:
     - If the product exists, increase its stock by the ordered quantity.
     - If the product no longer exists, recreate it with the price stored in the order.
4a. Order is already "cancelled" → System ignores further status changes.

---

### **Use Case: Access System State**
**Primary Actor:** Administrator/User
**Goal:** Retrieve current system state (users, products, stock, carts, orders).

**System Responsibilities:**
- Provide read-only access to:
  - Current users and their carts.
  - Products (name, price, **physical stock**).
  - Reservations and orders with statuses.

**Preconditions:**
- The system is operational.

**Postconditions:**
- The requested state is returned without modifications.

**Main Success Scenario:**
1. Actor requests system state (e.g., list of users).
2. System **provides read-only access to the data, including reservations and orders with their statuses, and physical stock (reservations are not subtracted from stock in this view).**

**Extensions:**
1a. Invalid request (e.g., non-existent entity) → System returns an empty or error response.

---

===== OUTPUT_review_architecture OUTPUT =====
{
  "score": 0.95,
  "issues": [
    "The 'reservations' attribute description in the Current Architecture does not explicitly state that reservations are tracked separately from physical stock, which could lead to ambiguity in understanding the inventory model. This should be clarified to match the feedback's requirement of not affecting physical stock until checkout.",
    "The 'checkout' method's special requirements mention 'If any reserved product no longer exists or lacks sufficient stock, the checkout must fail and no changes must be applied.' However, the validation step in the 'steps' section does not explicitly mention checking against 'stock excluding other reservations', which is a key requirement from the feedback.",
    "The 'get_system_state' method's description states 'Physical stock must not reflect reservations in this view,' but the 'special requirements' do not explicitly mention this, which could lead to implementation ambiguity.",
    "The 'add_to_cart' method's 'steps' section does not explicitly mention that the validation of available stock should exclude existing reservations, which is a critical requirement from the feedback.",
    "The 'checkout' method's 'steps' section does not explicitly state that stock updates (reduction) are the only time stock is modified, which is a key requirement from the feedback. This should be reinforced in the steps for clarity."
  ]
}

@classmethod
def create_user(cls, user_id):
    if not user_id or user_id in cls.users:
        return False

    cls.users[user_id] = {
        'cart': {},
        'reservations': {}
    }
    return True

===== generate_single_method OUTPUT =====
Generated code for EShopSystem.delete_user: 
@classmethod
def delete_user(cls, user_id):
    # Validate the user exists
    if user_id not in cls.users:
        return False

    # Process reservations
    user_reservations = cls.users[user_id]['reservations'].copy()
    for product_name, quantity in user_reservations.items():
        if product_name in cls.products:
            # Return reserved quantity to available stock
            cls.products[product_name]['stock'] += quantity
        # Remove reservation regardless of product existence
        del cls.users[user_id]['reservations'][product_name]

    # Clear cart and reservations
    cls.users[user_id]['cart'].clear()

    # Delete user
    del cls.users[user_id]

    return True
===============================

@classmethod
def add_product(cls, product_name, price, stock):
    if not product_name or price < 0 or stock < 0:
        return False

    if product_name in cls.products:
        cls.products[product_name]['stock'] += stock
        cls.products[product_name]['price'] = price
    else:
        cls.products[product_name] = {
            'price': price,
            'stock': stock
        }

    return True

===== generate_single_method OUTPUT =====
Generated code for EShopSystem.add_to_cart: 
def add_to_cart(self, user_id, product_name, quantity):
    # Check for zero quantity
    if quantity <= 0:
        return False

    # Validate user exists
    if user_id not in self.users:
        return False

    # Validate product exists and has sufficient stock
    if product_name not in self.products:
        return False

    # Calculate available stock (physical stock minus existing reservations)
    physical_stock = self.products[product_name]['stock']
    existing_reservations = sum(
        self.reservations[uid].get(product_name, 0)
        for uid in self.reservations
        if uid != user_id
    )
    available_stock = physical_stock - existing_reservations

    # Check if requested quantity is available
    if quantity > available_stock:
        return False

    # Update user's cart
    if product_name in self.users[user_id]['cart']:
        self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] += quantity
    else:
        self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] = quantity

    # Update user's reservation
    if user_id in self.reservations:
        if product_name in self.reservations[user_id]:
            self.reservations[user_id][product_name] += quantity
        else:
            self.reservations[user_id][product_name] = quantity
    else:
        self.reservations[user_id] = {product_name: quantity}

    return True
===============================


===== generate_single_method OUTPUT =====
Generated code for EShopSystem.remove_from_cart: 
def remove_from_cart(self, user_id, product_name, quantity):
    # Validate user exists
    if user_id not in self.users:
        return False

    # Validate product exists in user's cart
    user_cart = self.users[user_id]['cart']
    if product_name not in user_cart:
        return False

    # Validate quantity to remove is not more than reserved
    reserved_quantity = user_cart[product_name]
    if quantity > reserved_quantity:
        return False

    # Update cart
    if quantity == reserved_quantity:
        del user_cart[product_name]
    else:
        user_cart[product_name] -= quantity

    # Update reservation
    user_reservations = self.users[user_id]['reservations']
    if product_name in user_reservations:
        if quantity == user_reservations[product_name]:
            del user_reservations[product_name]
        else:
            user_reservations[product_name] -= quantity

    return True
===============================


===== generate_single_method OUTPUT =====
Generated code for EShopSystem.checkout: 
def checkout(self, user_id):
    # Step 1: Validate user exists and has non-empty cart
    if user_id not in self.users or not self.users[user_id]['cart']:
        return None

    cart = self.users[user_id]['cart']
    total_price = 0.0

    # Step 2: Validate all reserved products exist and have sufficient stock
    for product_name, quantity in cart.items():
        if product_name not in self.products:
            return None

        product = self.products[product_name]
        # Calculate available stock (stock - other reservations)
        other_reservations = sum(
            self.reservations[other_user][product_name]
            for other_user in self.reservations
            if other_user != user_id and product_name in self.reservations[other_user]
        )
        available_stock = product['stock'] - other_reservations

        if available_stock < quantity:
            return None

        total_price += product['price'] * quantity

    # Step 3: Generate order ID
    order_id = f"SK{self.next_order_id:05d}"
    self.next_order_id += 1

    # Step 4: Create order
    order = {
        'status': 'pending',
        'items': cart.copy(),
        'price_snapshot': {product: self.products[product]['price'] for product in cart}
    }
    self.orders[order_id] = order

    # Step 5: Update stock
    for product_name, quantity in cart.items():
        self.products[product_name]['stock'] -= quantity

    # Step 6: Clear cart and reservations
    self.users[user_id]['cart'].clear()
    if user_id in self.reservations:
        self.reservations[user_id].clear()

    return order_id
===============================


===== generate_single_method OUTPUT =====
Generated code for EShopSystem.update_order_status: 
def update_order_status(self, order_id, new_status):
    # Validate order exists
    if order_id not in self.orders:
        return False

    current_status = self.orders[order_id]['status']

    # Check if order is already in terminal state
    if current_status == 'cancelled' or current_status == 'delivered':
        return False

    # Validate status transition
    valid_transitions = {
        'pending': ['processing', 'cancelled'],
        'processing': ['completed', 'cancelled'],
        'completed': ['shipped', 'cancelled'],
        'shipped': ['delivered', 'cancelled']
    }

    if new_status not in valid_transitions.get(current_status, []):
        return False

    # Handle cancellation
    if new_status == 'cancelled':
        for product_name, quantity in self.orders[order_id]['items'].items():
            if product_name in self.products:
                # Restore stock for existing products
                self.products[product_name]['stock'] += quantity
            else:
                # Recreate product with price from order snapshot
                price = self.orders[order_id]['price_snapshot'].get(product_name, 0.0)
                self.products[product_name] = {
                    'price': price,
                    'stock': quantity
                }

    # Update order status
    self.orders[order_id]['status'] = new_status
    return True
===============================


===== generate_single_method OUTPUT =====
Generated code for EShopSystem.get_system_state: 
def get_system_state(self):
    """Retrieves the current system state, including users, products, physical stock, carts, and orders.

    Returns:
        dict: A read-only snapshot of the current system state, including:
            - users: Dictionary of user data including carts
            - products: Dictionary of product data including physical stock
            - reservations: Dictionary of user reservations
            - orders: Dictionary of all orders
            - next_order_id: The next available order ID
    """
    return {
        'users': {user_id: {
            'cart': user_data['cart'].copy(),
            'reservations': user_data['reservations'].copy()
        } for user_id, user_data in self.users.items()},
        'products': {product_name: product_data.copy()
                    for product_name, product_data in self.products.items()},
        'reservations': {user_id: reservations.copy()
                        for user_id, reservations in self.reservations.items()},
        'orders': {order_id: order_data.copy()
                  for order_id, order_data in self.orders.items()},
        'next_order_id': self.next_order_id
    }

def remove_from_cart(self, user_id, product_name, quantity):
    # Validate user exists
    if user_id not in self.users:
        return False

    # Validate product exists in user's cart
    user_cart = self.users[user_id]['cart']
    if product_name not in user_cart:
        return False

    # Validate quantity to remove is not more than reserved
    reserved_quantity = user_cart[product_name]
    if quantity > reserved_quantity:
        return False

    # Update cart
    if quantity == reserved_quantity:
        del user_cart[product_name]
    else:
        user_cart[product_name] -= quantity

    # Update reservation
    user_reservations = self.users[user_id]['reservations']
    if product_name in user_reservations:

            del user_reservations[product_name]

            if user_id in self.reservations and product_name in self.reservations[user_id]:
                self.reservations[user_id][product_name] -= quantity

def add_to_cart(self, user_id, product_name, quantity):
    # Check for zero quantity
    if quantity <= 0:
        return False

    # Validate user exists
    if user_id not in self.users:
        return False

def checkout(self, user_id):

def add_to_cart(self, user_id, product_name, quantity):
    # Check for zero quantity
    if quantity <= 0:
        return False

    # Validate user exists
    if user_id not in self.users:
        return False

    # Validate product exists and has positive physical stock
    if product_name not in self.products or self.products[product_name]['stock'] <= 0:
        return False

    # Calculate available stock (physical stock minus all existing reservations)
    physical_stock = self.products[product_name]['stock']
    existing_reservations = sum(
        self.reservations[uid].get(product_name, 0)
        for uid in self.reservations
    )

    # Check if requested quantity is available
    if quantity > (physical_stock - existing_reservations):
        return False

    # Update user's cart
    if product_name in self.users[user_id]['cart']:
        self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] += quantity
    else:
        self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] = quantity

    # Update user's reservation
    if user_id in self.reservations:
        if product_name in self.reservations[user_id]:
            self.reservations[user_id][product_name] += quantity
        else:
            self.reservations[user_id][product_name] = quantity
    else:
        self.reservations[user_id] = {product_name: quantity}

    return True

Regenerated code for EShopSystem.remove_from_cart: 
def remove_from_cart(self, user_id, product_name, quantity):
    # Validate user exists
    if user_id not in self.users:
        return False

    # Validate product exists in user's cart
    user_cart = self.users[user_id]['cart']
    if product_name not in user_cart:
        return False

    # Validate quantity to remove is not more than reserved
    reserved_quantity = user_cart[product_name]
    if quantity > reserved_quantity:
        return False

    # Update cart
    if quantity == reserved_quantity:
        del user_cart[product_name]
    else:
        user_cart[product_name] -= quantity

    # Update reservation
    user_reservations = self.users[user_id]['reservations']
    if product_name in user_reservations:
        if quantity == user_reservations[product_name]:
            del user_reservations[product_name]
            if user_id in self.reservations and product_name in self.reservations[user_id]:
                del self.reservations[user_id][product_name]
        else:
            user_reservations[product_name] -= quantity
            if user_id in self.reservations:
                if product_name in self.reservations[user_id]:
                    self.reservations[user_id][product_name] -= quantity
                else:
                    self.reservations[user_id][product_name] = user_reservations[product_name]

    return True
===============================


===== regenerate_single_method OUTPUT =====
Regenerated code for EShopSystem.checkout: 
def checkout(self, user_id):
    # Step 1: Validate user exists and has non-empty cart
    if user_id not in self.users or not self.users[user_id]['cart']:
        return None

    cart = self.users[user_id]['cart']
    total_price = 0.0

    # Step 2: Validate all reserved products exist and have sufficient stock
    for product_name, quantity in cart.items():
        if product_name not in self.products:
            return None

        product = self.products[product_name]
        # Calculate available stock (stock - other reservations)
        other_reservations = sum(
            self.reservations[other_user][product_name]
            for other_user in self.reservations
            if other_user != user_id and product_name in self.reservations[other_user]
        )
        available_stock = product['stock'] - other_reservations

        if available_stock < quantity:
            return None

        total_price += product['price'] * quantity

    # Step 3: Generate order ID
    order_id = f"SK{self.next_order_id:05d}"
    self.next_order_id += 1

    # Step 4: Create order
    order = {
        'status': 'pending',
        'items': cart.copy(),
        'price_snapshot': {product: self.products[product]['price'] for product in cart}
    }
    self.orders[order_id] = order

    # Step 5: Update stock
    for product_name, quantity in cart.items():
        self.products[product_name]['stock'] -= quantity

    # Step 6: Clear cart and reservations
    self.users[user_id]['cart'].clear()
    if user_id in self.reservations:
        self.reservations[user_id].clear()

    return order_id
===============================

def add_to_cart(self, user_id, product_name, quantity):
    # Check for zero quantity
    if quantity <= 0:
        return False

    # Validate user exists
    if user_id not in self.users:
        return False

    # Validate product exists and has positive physical stock
    if product_name not in self.products or self.products[product_name]['stock'] <= 0:
        return False

    # Calculate available stock (physical stock minus all existing reservations)
    physical_stock = self.products[product_name]['stock']
    existing_reservations = sum(
        self.users[uid]['reservations'].get(product_name, 0)
        for uid in self.users
        if product_name in self.users[uid]['reservations']
    )

    # Check if requested quantity is available
    if quantity > (physical_stock - existing_reservations):
        return False

    # Update user's cart
    if product_name in self.users[user_id]['cart']:
        self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] += quantity
    else:
        self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] = quantity

    # Update user's reservation
    if product_name in self.users[user_id]['reservations']:
        self.users[user_id]['reservations'][product_name] += quantity
    else:
        self.users[user_id]['reservations'][product_name] = quantity

    return True

===== regenerate_single_method OUTPUT =====
Regenerated code for EShopSystem.update_order_status: 
@classmethod
def update_order_status(cls, order_id, new_status):
    # Validate order exists
    if order_id not in cls.orders:
        return False

    current_status = cls.orders[order_id]['status']

    # Check if order is already in terminal state
    if current_status == 'cancelled' or current_status == 'delivered':
        return False

    # Validate status transition
    valid_transitions = {
        'pending': ['processing', 'cancelled'],
        'processing': ['completed', 'cancelled'],
        'completed': ['shipped', 'cancelled'],
        'shipped': ['delivered', 'cancelled']
    }

    if new_status not in valid_transitions.get(current_status, []):
        return False

    # Handle cancellation
    if new_status == 'cancelled':
        for product_name, quantity in cls.orders[order_id]['items'].items():
            if product_name in cls.products:
                # Restore stock for existing products
                cls.products[product_name]['stock'] += quantity
            else:
                # Recreate product with price from order snapshot
                price = cls.orders[order_id]['price_snapshot'].get(product_name, 0.0)
                cls.products[product_name] = {
                    'price': price,
                    'stock': quantity
                }

    # Update order status
    cls.orders[order_id]['status'] = new_status
    return True
===============================


===== regenerate_single_method OUTPUT =====
Regenerated code for EShopSystem.get_system_state: 
def get_system_state(self):
    """Retrieves the current system state, including users, products, physical stock, carts, and orders.

    Returns:
        dict: A read-only snapshot of the current system state, including:
            - users: Dictionary of user data including carts
            - products: Dictionary of product data including physical stock
            - reservations: Dictionary of user reservations
            - orders: Dictionary of all orders
            - next_order_id: The next available order ID
    """
    return {
        'users': {user_id: {
            'cart': user_data['cart'].copy(),
            'reservations': user_data['reservations'].copy()
        } for user_id, user_data in self.users.items()},
        'products': {product_name: product_data.copy()
                    for product_name, product_data in self.products.items()},
        'reservations': {user_id: reservations.copy()
                        for user_id, reservations in self.reservations.items()},
        'orders': {order_id: order_data.copy()
                  for order_id, order_data in self.orders.items()},
        'next_order_id': self.next_order_id
    }

def checkout(self, user_id):
    # Step 1: Validate user exists and has non-empty cart
    if user_id not in self.users or not self.users[user_id]['cart']:
        return None

    cart = self.users[user_id]['cart']
    total_price = 0.0

    # Step 2: Validate all reserved products exist and have sufficient stock
    for product_name, quantity in cart.items():

            return None

        product = self.products[product_name]
        # Calculate available stock (stock - other reservations)
        other_reservations = sum(

            for other_user in self.reservations
            if other_user != user_id
        )

        if available_stock < quantity:

Regenerated code for EShopSystem.delete_user: 
def delete_user(self, user_id):
    # Validate the user exists
    if user_id not in self.users:
        return False

    # Process reservations
    user_reservations = self.users[user_id]['reservations'].copy()
    for product_name, quantity in user_reservations.items():
        if product_name in self.products:
            # Return reserved quantity to available stock
            self.products[product_name]['stock'] += quantity
        # Remove reservation regardless of product existence
        del self.users[user_id]['reservations'][product_name]

    # Clear cart and reservations
    self.users[user_id]['cart'].clear()

    # Delete user
    del self.users[user_id]

    return True

Regenerated code for EShopSystem.remove_from_cart: 
def remove_from_cart(self, user_id, product_name, quantity):
    # Validate user exists
    if user_id not in self.users:
        return False

    # Validate product exists in user's cart
    user_cart = self.users[user_id]['cart']
    if product_name not in user_cart:
        return False

    # Validate quantity to remove is not more than reserved
    reserved_quantity = user_cart[product_name]
    if quantity > reserved_quantity:
        return False

    # Update cart
    if quantity == reserved_quantity:
        del user_cart[product_name]
    else:
        user_cart[product_name] -= quantity

    # Update reservation
    user_reservations = self.users[user_id]['reservations']
    if product_name in user_reservations:
        if quantity == user_reservations[product_name]:
            del user_reservations[product_name]
        else:
            user_reservations[product_name] -= quantity

    return True

===== regenerate_single_method OUTPUT =====
Regenerated code for EShopSystem.checkout: 
def checkout(self, user_id):
    # Step 1: Validate user exists and has non-empty cart
    if user_id not in self.users or not self.users[user_id]['cart']:
        return None

    cart = self.users[user_id]['cart']
    total_price = 0.0

    # Step 2: Validate all reserved products exist and have sufficient stock
    for product_name, quantity in cart.items():
        if product_name not in self.products:
            return None

        product = self.products[product_name]
        # Calculate available stock (stock - other reservations)
        other_reservations = sum(
            self.users.get(other_user, {}).get('reservations', {}).get(product_name, 0)
            for other_user in self.users
            if other_user != user_id
        )
        available_stock = product['stock'] - other_reservations

        if available_stock < quantity:
            return None

        total_price += product['price'] * quantity

    # Step 3: Generate order ID
    order_id = f"SK{self.next_order_id:05d}"
    self.next_order_id += 1

    # Step 4: Create order
    order = {
        'status': 'pending',
        'items': cart.copy(),
        'price_snapshot': {product: self.products[product]['price'] for product in cart}
    }
    self.orders[order_id] = order

    # Step 5: Update stock
    for product_name, quantity in cart.items():
        self.products[product_name]['stock'] -= quantity

    # Step 6: Clear cart and reservations
    self.users[user_id]['cart'].clear()
    if user_id in self.users and 'reservations' in self.users[user_id]:
        self.users[user_id]['reservations'].clear()

    return order_id

===== regenerate_single_method OUTPUT =====
Regenerated code for EShopSystem.get_system_state: 
def get_system_state(self):
    """Retrieves the current system state, including users, products, physical stock, carts, and orders.

    Returns:
        dict: A read-only snapshot of the current system state, including:
            - users: Dictionary of user data including carts
            - products: Dictionary of product data including physical stock
            - reservations: Dictionary of user reservations
            - orders: Dictionary of all orders
            - next_order_id: The next available order ID
    """
    return {
        'users': {user_id: {
            'cart': user_data['cart'].copy(),
            'reservations': user_data['reservations'].copy()
        } for user_id, user_data in self.users.items()},
        'products': {product_name: product_data.copy()
                    for product_name, product_data in self.products.items()},
        'orders': {order_id: order_data.copy()
                  for order_id, order_data in self.orders.items()},
        'next_order_id': self.next_order_id
    }

===== assemble_program OUTPUT =====
from copy import deepcopy
from types import MappingProxyType

class EShopSystem:
    def __init__(self):
        self.users = {}
        self.products = {}
        self.orders = {}
        self.next_order_id = 1
        self.reservations = {}

    def create_user(self, user_id):
        if not user_id or user_id in self.users:
            return False

        self.users[user_id] = {
            'cart': {},
            'reservations': {}
        }
        return True

    def add_product(self, product_name, price, stock):
        if not product_name or price < 0 or stock < 0:
            return False

        if product_name in self.products:
            self.products[product_name]['stock'] += stock
            self.products[product_name]['price'] = price
        else:
            self.products[product_name] = {
                'price': price,
                'stock': stock
            }

        return True

    def delete_product(self, product_name):
        if product_name not in self.products:
            return False

        del self.products[product_name]
        return True

    def get_system_state(self):
        """Retrieves the current system state, including users, products, physical stock, carts, and orders.

        Returns:
            dict: A read-only snapshot of the current system state, including:
                - users: Dictionary of user data including carts
                - products: Dictionary of product data including physical stock
                - reservations: Dictionary of user reservations
                - orders: Dictionary of all orders
                - next_order_id: The next available order ID
        """
        state = {
            'users': deepcopy(self.users),
            'products': deepcopy(self.products),
            'orders': deepcopy(self.orders),
            'next_order_id': self.next_order_id,
            'reservations': deepcopy(self.reservations)
        }
        return MappingProxyType(state)

    def delete_user(self, user_id):
        if user_id not in self.users:
            return False

        # Process reservations
        user_reservations = self.users[user_id]['reservations'].copy()
        for product_name, quantity in user_reservations.items():
            if product_name in self.products:
                self.products[product_name]['stock'] += quantity
            del self.users[user_id]['reservations'][product_name]

        # Clear cart and reservations
        self.users[user_id]['cart'].clear()

        # Delete user
        del self.users[user_id]

        return True

    def add_to_cart(self, user_id, product_name, quantity):
        if quantity <= 0:
            return False

        if user_id not in self.users:
            return False

        if product_name not in self.products or self.products[product_name]['stock'] <= 0:
            return False

        physical_stock = self.products[product_name]['stock']
        existing_reservations = sum(
            self.users[uid]['reservations'].get(product_name, 0)
            for uid in self.users
            if product_name in self.users[uid]['reservations']
        )

        if quantity > (physical_stock - existing_reservations):
            return False

        if product_name in self.users[user_id]['cart']:
            self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] += quantity
        else:
            self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] = quantity

        if product_name in self.users[user_id]['reservations']:
            self.users[user_id]['reservations'][product_name] += quantity
        else:
            self.users[user_id]['reservations'][product_name] = quantity

        return True

    def remove_from_cart(self, user_id, product_name, quantity):
        if user_id not in self.users:
            return False

        user_cart = self.users[user_id]['cart']
        if product_name not in user_cart:
            return False

        reserved_quantity = user_cart[product_name]
        if quantity > reserved_quantity:
            return False

        if quantity == reserved_quantity:
            del user_cart[product_name]
        else:
            user_cart[product_name] -= quantity

        user_reservations = self.users[user_id]['reservations']
        if product_name in user_reservations:
            if quantity == user_reservations[product_name]:
                del user_reservations[product_name]
            else:
                user_reservations[product_name] -= quantity

        return True

    def checkout(self, user_id):
        if user_id not in self.users or not self.users[user_id]['cart']:
            return None

        cart = self.users[user_id]['cart']
        total_price = 0.0

        for product_name, quantity in cart.items():
            if product_name not in self.products:
                return None

            product = self.products[product_name]
            other_reservations = sum(
                self.users.get(other_user, {}).get('reservations', {}).get(product_name, 0)
                for other_user in self.users
                if other_user != user_id
            )
            available_stock = product['stock'] - other_reservations

            if available_stock < quantity:
                return None

            total_price += product['price'] * quantity

        order_id = f"SK{self.next_order_id:05d}"
        self.next_order_id += 1

        order = {
            'status': 'pending',
            'items': cart.copy(),
            'price_snapshot': {product: self.products[product]['price'] for product in cart},
            'total_price': total_price
        }
        self.orders[order_id] = order

        for product_name, quantity in cart.items():
            self.products[product_name]['stock'] -= quantity

        self.users[user_id]['cart'].clear()
        if user_id in self.users and 'reservations' in self.users[user_id]:
            self.users[user_id]['reservations'].clear()

        return order_id

    def update_order_status(self, order_id, new_status):
        if order_id not in self.orders:
            return False

        current_status = self.orders[order_id]['status']

        if current_status in ['cancelled', 'delivered']:
            return False

        valid_transitions = {
            'pending': ['processing', 'cancelled'],
            'processing': ['completed', 'cancelled'],
            'completed': ['shipped', 'cancelled'],
            'shipped': ['delivered', 'cancelled']
        }

        if new_status not in valid_transitions.get(current_status, []):
            return False

        if new_status == 'cancelled':
            for product_name, quantity in self.orders[order_id]['items'].items():
                if product_name in self.products:
                    self.products[product_name]['stock'] += quantity
                else:
                    price = self.orders[order_id]['price_snapshot'].get(product_name, 0.0)
                    self.products[product_name] = {
                        'price': price,
                        'stock': quantity
                    }

        self.orders[order_id]['status'] = new_status
        return True

def main():
    # Create an instance of the e-shop system
    shop = EShopSystem()

    # Demonstrate user management
    print("=== User Management ===")
    shop.create_user("user1")
    shop.create_user("user2")
    print("Created users:", list(shop.users.keys()))

    # Demonstrate product management
    print("\n=== Product Management ===")
    shop.add_product("Laptop", 999.99, 10)
    shop.add_product("Phone", 699.99, 15)
    print("Available products:", list(shop.products.keys()))

    # Demonstrate adding to cart
    print("\n=== Cart Operations ===")
    shop.add_to_cart("user1", "Laptop", 2)
    shop.add_to_cart("user1", "Phone", 1)
    print("User1's cart:", shop.users["user1"]["cart"])

    # Demonstrate checkout
    print("\n=== Checkout ===")
    order_id = shop.checkout("user1")
    print(f"Order created with ID: {order_id}")
    print("User1's cart after checkout:", shop.users["user1"]["cart"])

    # Demonstrate order status update
    print("\n=== Order Status Update ===")
    shop.update_order_status(order_id, "processing")
    print(f"Order {order_id} status:", shop.orders[order_id]["status"])

    # Demonstrate system state
    print("\n=== System State ===")
    state = shop.get_system_state()
    print("Users:", state["users"])
    print("Products:", state["products"])
    print("Orders:", state["orders"])

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

===== USE_CASE_EXTRACTION_FAILED OUTPUT =====
Here's the structured Use Case derived from the provided program:

---

**Use Case: Manage E-Shop Operations**

**Primary Actor:** User (Customer, Administrator)

**Goal:**
- Manage user accounts, products, shopping carts, and orders in an e-shop system.
- Enable users to browse products, add/remove items from cart, and checkout.
- Allow administrators to manage products, users, and order statuses.

---

**System Responsibilities:**
1. **User Management**
   - Create new user accounts with unique identifiers.
   - Delete existing user accounts and release associated resources (e.g., reservations, cart items).
   - Prevent duplicate user accounts.

2. **Product Management**
   - Add new products with name, price, and stock quantity.
   - Update existing product stock and price.
   - Delete products from the system.
   - Ensure product stock is non-negative and price is valid.

3. **Cart Management**
   - Allow users to add products to their shopping cart with specified quantities.
   - Reserve product stock for items in the cart to prevent overselling.
   - Enable users to remove items from their cart and release reserved stock.
   - Validate product availability before adding to cart.

4. **Order Processing**
   - Process user checkout to create orders with unique identifiers.
   - Calculate total order price based on current product prices.
   - Deduct ordered items from product stock upon successful checkout.
   - Clear user cart and reservations after checkout.
   - Update order status with valid transitions (e.g., pending → processing → completed → shipped → delivered).
   - Cancel orders and restore product stock if applicable.

5. **System State Monitoring**
   - Provide a read-only snapshot of the current system state, including users, products, orders, and reservations.

---

**Preconditions:**
1. The e-shop system is initialized and operational.
2. For user-specific actions (e.g., add to cart, checkout), the user must exist in the system.
3. For product-specific actions (e.g., add to cart), the product must exist and have available stock.
4. For order status updates, the order must exist and not be in a terminal state (e.g., cancelled, delivered).

---

**Postconditions:**
1. **User Management**
   - New user accounts are created with empty carts and reservations.
   - Deleted user accounts are removed, and their reserved stock is released.

2. **Product Management**
   - New products are added with specified price and stock.
   - Existing products have updated stock or price.
   - Deleted products are removed from the system.

3. **Cart Management**
   - Products are added to the user’s cart with reserved stock.
   - Removed items are deleted from the cart, and reserved stock is released.

4. **Order Processing**
   - Orders are created with a unique ID, pending status, and price snapshot.
   - Product stock is deducted for completed orders.
   - Order status is updated, and stock is restored if the order is cancelled.

5. **System State Monitoring**
   - A read-only snapshot of the system state is provided without altering the system.

---

**Main Success Scenario:**

1. **Create User**
   - *Actor:* Administrator
   - *System:* Validates the user ID is unique and non-empty.
   - *System:* Creates a new user account with an empty cart and reservations.
   - *System:* Confirms successful creation.

2. **Add Product**
   - *Actor:* Administrator
   - *System:* Validates the product name is non-empty, price is non-negative, and stock is non-negative.
   - *System:* Adds the product to the system or updates stock/price if the product exists.
   - *System:* Confirms successful addition/update.

3. **Add to Cart**
   - *Actor:* User
   - *System:* Validates the user exists, the product exists, and sufficient stock is available (accounting for other reservations).
   - *System:* Adds the product to the user’s cart and reserves the stock.
   - *System:* Confirms successful addition.

4. **Remove from Cart**
   - *Actor:* User
   - *System:* Validates the user and product exist in the cart.
   - *System:* Removes the specified quantity from the cart and releases the reserved stock.
   - *System:* Confirms successful removal.

5. **Checkout**
   - *Actor:* User
   - *System:* Validates the user exists, the cart is non-empty, and all products have sufficient stock (accounting for other reservations).
   - *System:* Calculates the total order price.
   - *System:* Creates an order with a unique ID, pending status, and price snapshot.
   - *System:* Deducts ordered items from product stock.
   - *System:* Clears the user’s cart and reservations.
   - *System:* Returns the order ID to the user.

6. **Update Order Status**
   - *Actor:* Administrator
   - *System:* Validates the order exists and the new status is a valid transition from the current status.
   - *System:* Updates the order status.
   - *System:* If cancelling, restores product stock for the order items.
   - *System:* Confirms successful status update.

7. **View System State**
   - *Actor:* Administrator
   - *System:* Retrieves a read-only snapshot of users, products, orders, reservations, and the next order ID.
   - *System:* Returns the snapshot to the actor.

---

**Extensions:**

1. **Create User**
   - *1a. User ID is invalid or duplicate:*
     - *System:* Rejects the request and notifies the actor.

2. **Add Product**
   - *2a. Product details are invalid (e.g., negative price/stock):*
     - *System:* Rejects the request and notifies the actor.

3. **Add to Cart**
   - *3a. User does not exist:*
     - *System:* Rejects the request and notifies the actor.
   - *3b. Product does not exist or has insufficient stock:*
     - *System:* Rejects the request and notifies the actor.
   - *3c. Requested quantity exceeds available stock (after reservations):*
     - *System:* Rejects the request and notifies the actor.

4. **Remove from Cart**
   - *4a. User or product does not exist in the cart:*
     - *System:* Rejects the request and notifies the actor.
   - *4b. Requested quantity exceeds cart quantity:*
     - *System:* Rejects the request and notifies the actor.

5. **Checkout**
   - *5a. User does not exist or cart is empty:*
     - *System:* Rejects the request and notifies the actor.
   - *5b. Product in cart has insufficient stock (after reservations):*
     - *System:* Rejects the request and notifies the actor.

6. **Update Order Status**
   - *6a. Order does not exist:*
     - *System:* Rejects the request and notifies the actor.
   - *6b. Order is in a terminal state (cancelled/delivered):*
     - *System:* Rejects the request and notifies the actor.
   - *6c. Invalid status transition:*
     - *System:* Rejects the request and notifies the actor.

7. **Delete User**
   - *7a. User does not exist:*
     - *System:* Rejects the request and notifies the actor.
   - *7b. User has active reservations:*
     - *System:* Releases all reserved stock before deleting the user.

8. **Delete Product**
   - *8a. Product does not exist:*
     - *System:* Rejects the request and notifies the actor.

---

**Special Requirements:**
1. **Stock Reservation:**
   - The system must reserve product stock when items are added to a cart to prevent overselling.
   - Reserved stock must be released if items are removed from the cart or the user is deleted.

2. **Order Status Transitions:**
   - The system must enforce valid order status transitions (e.g., pending → processing, but not pending → delivered).

3. **Data Integrity:**
   - The system must ensure product stock and order statuses are consistent after operations (e.g., checkout, cancellation).

4. **Read-Only State:**
   - The system state snapshot must be read-only to prevent external modifications.

===== assemble_program OUTPUT =====
from copy import deepcopy
from types import MappingProxyType

class EShopSystem:
    def __init__(self):
        self.users = {}
        self.products = {}
        self.orders = {}
        self.next_order_id = 1
        self.reservations = {}

    def create_user(self, user_id):
        if not user_id or user_id in self.users:
            return False

        self.users[user_id] = {
            'cart': {},
            'reservations': {}
        }
        return True

    def add_product(self, product_name, price, stock):
        if not product_name or price < 0 or stock < 0:
            return False

        if product_name in self.products:
            self.products[product_name]['stock'] += stock
            self.products[product_name]['price'] = price
        else:
            self.products[product_name] = {
                'price': price,
                'stock': stock
            }

        return True

    def delete_product(self, product_name):
        if product_name not in self.products:
            return False

        del self.products[product_name]
        return True

    def get_system_state(self):
        """Retrieves the current system state, including users, products, physical stock, carts, and orders.

        Returns:
            dict: A read-only snapshot of the current system state, including:
                - users: Dictionary of user data including carts
                - products: Dictionary of product data including physical stock
                - reservations: Dictionary of user reservations
                - orders: Dictionary of all orders
                - next_order_id: The next available order ID
        """
        state = {
            'users': deepcopy(self.users),
            'products': deepcopy(self.products),
            'orders': deepcopy(self.orders),
            'next_order_id': self.next_order_id,
            'reservations': deepcopy(self.reservations)
        }
        return MappingProxyType(state)

    def delete_user(self, user_id):
        if user_id not in self.users:
            return False

        # Process reservations
        user_reservations = self.users[user_id]['reservations'].copy()
        for product_name, quantity in user_reservations.items():
            if product_name in self.products:
                self.products[product_name]['stock'] += quantity
            del self.users[user_id]['reservations'][product_name]

        # Clear cart and reservations
        self.users[user_id]['cart'].clear()

        # Delete user
        del self.users[user_id]

        return True

    def add_to_cart(self, user_id, product_name, quantity):
        if quantity <= 0:
            return False

        if user_id not in self.users:
            return False

        if product_name not in self.products:
            return False

        physical_stock = self.products[product_name]['stock']
        existing_reservations = sum(
            self.users[uid]['reservations'].get(product_name, 0)
            for uid in self.users
            if product_name in self.users[uid]['reservations']
        )

        if quantity > (physical_stock - existing_reservations):
            return False

        if product_name in self.users[user_id]['cart']:
            self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] += quantity
        else:
            self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] = quantity

        if product_name in self.users[user_id]['reservations']:
            self.users[user_id]['reservations'][product_name] += quantity
        else:
            self.users[user_id]['reservations'][product_name] = quantity

        return True

    def remove_from_cart(self, user_id, product_name, quantity):
        if user_id not in self.users:
            return False

        user_cart = self.users[user_id]['cart']
        if product_name not in user_cart:
            return False

        reserved_quantity = user_cart[product_name]
        if quantity > reserved_quantity:
            return False

        if quantity == reserved_quantity:
            del user_cart[product_name]
        else:
            user_cart[product_name] -= quantity

        user_reservations = self.users[user_id]['reservations']
        if product_name in user_reservations:
            if quantity == user_reservations[product_name]:
                del user_reservations[product_name]
            else:
                user_reservations[product_name] -= quantity

        return True

    def checkout(self, user_id):
        if user_id not in self.users or not self.users[user_id]['cart']:
            return None

        cart = self.users[user_id]['cart']
        total_price = 0.0

        for product_name, quantity in cart.items():
            if product_name not in self.products:
                return None

            product = self.products[product_name]
            other_reservations = sum(
                self.users.get(other_user, {}).get('reservations', {}).get(product_name, 0)
                for other_user in self.users
                if other_user != user_id
            )
            available_stock = product['stock'] - other_reservations

            if available_stock < quantity:
                return None

            total_price += product['price'] * quantity

        order_id = f"SK{self.next_order_id:05d}"
        self.next_order_id += 1

        order = {
            'status': 'pending',
            'items': cart.copy(),
            'price_snapshot': {product: self.products[product]['price'] for product in cart},
            'total_price': total_price
        }
        self.orders[order_id] = order

        for product_name, quantity in cart.items():
            self.products[product_name]['stock'] -= quantity

        self.users[user_id]['cart'].clear()
        if user_id in self.users and 'reservations' in self.users[user_id]:
            self.users[user_id]['reservations'].clear()

        return order_id

    def update_order_status(self, order_id, new_status):
        if order_id not in self.orders:
            return False

        current_status = self.orders[order_id]['status']

        if current_status in ['cancelled', 'delivered']:
            return False

        valid_transitions = {
            'pending': ['processing', 'cancelled'],
            'processing': ['completed', 'cancelled'],
            'completed': ['shipped', 'cancelled'],
            'shipped': ['delivered', 'cancelled']
        }

        if new_status not in valid_transitions.get(current_status, []):
            return False

        if new_status == 'cancelled':
            for product_name, quantity in self.orders[order_id]['items'].items():
                if product_name in self.products:
                    self.products[product_name]['stock'] += quantity
                else:
                    price = self.orders[order_id]['price_snapshot'].get(product_name, 0.0)
                    self.products[product_name] = {
                        'price': price,
                        'stock': quantity
                    }

        self.orders[order_id]['status'] = new_status
        return True

def main():
    # Create an instance of the e-shop system
    shop = EShopSystem()

    # Demonstrate user management
    print("=== User Management ===")
    shop.create_user("user1")
    shop.create_user("user2")
    print("Created users:", list(shop.users.keys()))

    # Demonstrate product management
    print("\n=== Product Management ===")
    shop.add_product("Laptop", 999.99, 10)
    shop.add_product("Phone", 699.99, 15)
    print("Available products:", list(shop.products.keys()))

    # Demonstrate adding to cart
    print("\n=== Cart Operations ===")
    shop.add_to_cart("user1", "Laptop", 2)
    shop.add_to_cart("user1", "Phone", 1)
    print("User1's cart:", shop.users["user1"]["cart"])

    # Demonstrate checkout
    print("\n=== Checkout ===")
    order_id = shop.checkout("user1")
    print(f"Order created with ID: {order_id}")
    print("User1's cart after checkout:", shop.users["user1"]["cart"])

    # Demonstrate order status update
    print("\n=== Order Status Update ===")
    shop.update_order_status(order_id, "processing")
    print(f"Order {order_id} status:", shop.orders[order_id]["status"])

    # Demonstrate system state
    print("\n=== System State ===")
    state = shop.get_system_state()
    print("Users:", state["users"])
    print("Products:", state["products"])
    print("Orders:", state["orders"])

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

===== USE_CASE_EXTRACTION_FAILED OUTPUT =====
Here's the structured Use Case derived from the provided program:

---

**Use Case: Manage E-Shop Operations**

**Primary Actor:** Customer, Administrator

**Goal:**
- Customer: Purchase products, manage shopping cart, and track orders
- Administrator: Manage product inventory, monitor system state, and process orders

---

**System Responsibilities:**
1. **User Management**
   - Register new users in the system
   - Remove existing users and release their reserved inventory
   - Maintain user-specific shopping carts and reservations

2. **Product Management**
   - Add new products or update existing product stock and prices
   - Remove products from inventory
   - Track physical stock and reserved quantities across all users

3. **Shopping Cart Operations**
   - Allow users to add products to their cart with quantity validation
   - Enable users to remove products from their cart
   - Reserve inventory for cart items to prevent overselling

4. **Order Processing**
   - Process user checkouts and generate orders
   - Calculate total order price based on current product prices
   - Update inventory upon successful order creation
   - Support order status transitions with validation
   - Handle order cancellations and restock inventory

5. **System Monitoring**
   - Provide a read-only snapshot of the current system state, including users, products, orders, and reservations

---

**Preconditions:**
1. The e-shop system is initialized and operational
2. For user-specific actions, the user must exist in the system
3. For product-specific actions, the product must exist in the system (unless adding a new product)
4. For order-specific actions, the order must exist in the system

---

**Postconditions:**
1. **User Management**
   - New users are registered with empty carts and reservations
   - Deleted users are removed, and their reserved inventory is released back to stock

2. **Product Management**
   - New products are added to inventory with specified price and stock
   - Existing products have updated stock and/or price
   - Deleted products are removed from inventory

3. **Shopping Cart Operations**
   - Products are added to/removed from the user’s cart with corresponding inventory reservations
   - Inventory reservations are adjusted to reflect cart changes

4. **Order Processing**
   - Orders are created with unique IDs, itemized lists, and total price
   - Inventory is deducted for successfully created orders
   - Order status is updated according to valid transitions
   - Cancelled orders restock the associated inventory

5. **System Monitoring**
   - A consistent, read-only snapshot of the system state is provided

---

**Main Success Scenario:**

1. **Register User**
   - *Actor:* Administrator
   - *System:* Validates the user ID is unique and not empty, then registers the user with an empty cart and reservations.

2. **Add Product to Inventory**
   - *Actor:* Administrator
   - *System:* Validates the product name is not empty, price and stock are non-negative, then adds the product to inventory or updates existing product stock/price.

3. **Add Product to Cart**
   - *Actor:* Customer
   - *System:*
     - Validates the user exists, the product exists, and the requested quantity is available (considering other reservations).
     - Adds the product to the user’s cart and reserves the quantity in inventory.

4. **Remove Product from Cart**
   - *Actor:* Customer
   - *System:*
     - Validates the user and product exist in the cart.
     - Removes the specified quantity from the cart and releases the corresponding inventory reservation.

5. **Checkout**
   - *Actor:* Customer
   - *System:*
     - Validates the user exists and the cart is not empty.
     - Verifies sufficient stock is available (excluding other users' reservations).
     - Calculates the total order price.
     - Generates a unique order ID and creates the order with status "pending."
     - Deducts the ordered quantities from inventory and clears the user’s cart and reservations.
     - Returns the order ID to the customer.

6. **Update Order Status**
   - *Actor:* Administrator
   - *System:*
     - Validates the order exists and the new status is a valid transition from the current status.
     - Updates the order status.
     - If the new status is "cancelled," restocks the order items back to inventory.

7. **View System State**
   - *Actor:* Administrator
   - *System:* Returns a read-only snapshot of the current system state, including users, products, orders, and reservations.

---

**Extensions:**

1. **Register User**
   - *1a. User ID is invalid or already exists*
     - *System:* Rejects the request and notifies the actor.

2. **Add Product to Inventory**
   - *2a. Product details are invalid (empty name, negative price/stock)*
     - *System:* Rejects the request and notifies the actor.

3. **Add Product to Cart**
   - *3a. User does not exist*
     - *System:* Rejects the request and notifies the actor.
   - *3b. Product does not exist*
     - *System:* Rejects the request and notifies the actor.
   - *3c. Requested quantity exceeds available stock (considering other reservations)*
     - *System:* Rejects the request and notifies the actor.

4. **Remove Product from Cart**
   - *4a. User does not exist*
     - *System:* Rejects the request and notifies the actor.
   - *4b. Product does not exist in the cart*
     - *System:* Rejects the request and notifies the actor.
   - *4c. Requested quantity exceeds cart quantity*
     - *System:* Rejects the request and notifies the actor.

5. **Checkout**
   - *5a. User does not exist or cart is empty*
     - *System:* Rejects the request and notifies the actor.
   - *5b. Insufficient stock available for one or more items*
     - *System:* Rejects the request and notifies the actor.

6. **Update Order Status**
   - *6a. Order does not exist*
     - *System:* Rejects the request and notifies the actor.
   - *6b. Invalid status transition (e.g., from "delivered" to "processing")*
     - *System:* Rejects the request and notifies the actor.

7. **Delete User**
   - *7a. User does not exist*
     - *System:* Rejects the request and notifies the actor.

8. **Delete Product**
   - *8a. Product does not exist*
     - *System:* Rejects the request and notifies the actor.

---

**Special Requirements:**
1. **Inventory Management**
   - The system must prevent overselling by reserving inventory for cart items and validating stock during checkout.

2. **Order Status Transitions**
   - The system must enforce valid status transitions (e.g., "pending" → "processing" → "completed" → "shipped" → "delivered" or "cancelled").

3. **Data Consistency**
   - The system must ensure consistency between carts, reservations, and physical inventory at all times.

4. **Read-Only System State**
   - The system state snapshot must be immutable to prevent external modifications.

---

===== assemble_program OUTPUT =====
from copy import deepcopy
from types import MappingProxyType

class EShopSystem:
    def __init__(self):
        self.users = {}
        self.products = {}
        self.orders = {}
        self.next_order_id = 1
        self.reservations = {}

    def create_user(self, user_id):
        if not user_id or user_id in self.users:
            return False

        self.users[user_id] = {
            'cart': {},
            'reservations': {}
        }
        return True

    def add_product(self, product_name, price, stock):
        if not product_name or price < 0 or stock < 0:
            return False

        if product_name in self.products:
            self.products[product_name]['stock'] += stock
            self.products[product_name]['price'] = price
        else:
            self.products[product_name] = {
                'price': price,
                'stock': stock
            }

        return True

    def delete_product(self, product_name):
        if product_name not in self.products:
            return False

        del self.products[product_name]
        return True

    def get_system_state(self):
        """Retrieves the current system state, including users, products, physical stock, carts, and orders.

        Returns:
            dict: A read-only snapshot of the current system state, including:
                - users: Dictionary of user data including carts
                - products: Dictionary of product data including physical stock
                - reservations: Dictionary of user reservations
                - orders: Dictionary of all orders
                - next_order_id: The next available order ID
        """
        state = {
            'users': deepcopy(self.users),
            'products': deepcopy(self.products),
            'orders': deepcopy(self.orders),
            'next_order_id': self.next_order_id,
            'reservations': deepcopy(self.reservations)
        }
        return MappingProxyType(state)

    def delete_user(self, user_id):
        if user_id not in self.users:
            return False

        # Process reservations
        user_reservations = self.users[user_id]['reservations'].copy()
        for product_name, quantity in user_reservations.items():
            if product_name in self.products:
                self.products[product_name]['stock'] += quantity
            del self.users[user_id]['reservations'][product_name]

        # Clear cart and reservations
        self.users[user_id]['cart'].clear()

        # Delete user
        del self.users[user_id]

        return True

    def add_to_cart(self, user_id, product_name, quantity):
        if quantity <= 0:
            return False

        if user_id not in self.users:
            return False

        if product_name not in self.products:
            return False

        physical_stock = self.products[product_name]['stock']
        existing_reservations = sum(
            self.users[uid]['reservations'].get(product_name, 0)
            for uid in self.users
            if product_name in self.users[uid]['reservations']
        )

        if quantity > (physical_stock - existing_reservations):
            return False

        if product_name in self.users[user_id]['cart']:
            self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] += quantity
        else:
            self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] = quantity

        if product_name in self.users[user_id]['reservations']:
            self.users[user_id]['reservations'][product_name] += quantity
        else:
            self.users[user_id]['reservations'][product_name] = quantity

        return True

    def remove_from_cart(self, user_id, product_name, quantity):
        if user_id not in self.users:
            return False

        user_cart = self.users[user_id]['cart']
        if product_name not in user_cart:
            return False

        reserved_quantity = user_cart[product_name]
        if quantity > reserved_quantity:
            return False

        if quantity == reserved_quantity:
            del user_cart[product_name]
        else:
            user_cart[product_name] -= quantity

        user_reservations = self.users[user_id]['reservations']
        if product_name in user_reservations:
            if quantity == user_reservations[product_name]:
                del user_reservations[product_name]
            else:
                user_reservations[product_name] -= quantity

        return True

    def checkout(self, user_id):
        if user_id not in self.users or not self.users[user_id]['cart']:
            return None

        cart = self.users[user_id]['cart']
        total_price = 0.0

        for product_name, quantity in cart.items():
            if product_name not in self.products:
                return None

            product = self.products[product_name]
            other_reservations = sum(
                self.users.get(other_user, {}).get('reservations', {}).get(product_name, 0)
                for other_user in self.users
                if other_user != user_id
            )
            available_stock = product['stock'] - other_reservations

            if available_stock < quantity:
                return None

            total_price += product['price'] * quantity

        order_id = f"SK{self.next_order_id:05d}"
        self.next_order_id += 1

        order = {
            'status': 'pending',
            'items': cart.copy(),
            'price_snapshot': {product: self.products[product]['price'] for product in cart},
            'total_price': total_price
        }
        self.orders[order_id] = order

        for product_name, quantity in cart.items():
            self.products[product_name]['stock'] -= quantity

        self.users[user_id]['cart'].clear()
        if user_id in self.users and 'reservations' in self.users[user_id]:
            self.users[user_id]['reservations'].clear()

        return order_id

    def update_order_status(self, order_id, new_status):
        if order_id not in self.orders:
            return False

        current_status = self.orders[order_id]['status']

        if current_status in ['cancelled', 'delivered']:
            if new_status != 'cancelled':
                return False

        valid_transitions = {
            'pending': ['processing', 'cancelled'],
            'processing': ['completed', 'cancelled'],
            'completed': ['shipped', 'cancelled'],
            'shipped': ['delivered', 'cancelled']
        }

        if new_status not in valid_transitions.get(current_status, []):
            return False

        if new_status == 'cancelled':
            for product_name, quantity in self.orders[order_id]['items'].items():
                if product_name in self.products:
                    self.products[product_name]['stock'] += quantity
                else:
                    price = self.orders[order_id]['price_snapshot'].get(product_name, 0.0)
                    self.products[product_name] = {
                        'price': price,
                        'stock': quantity
                    }

        self.orders[order_id]['status'] = new_status
        return True

def main():
    # Create an instance of the e-shop system
    shop = EShopSystem()

    # Demonstrate user management
    print("=== User Management ===")
    shop.create_user("user1")
    shop.create_user("user2")
    print("Created users:", list(shop.users.keys()))

    # Demonstrate product management
    print("\n=== Product Management ===")
    shop.add_product("Laptop", 999.99, 10)
    shop.add_product("Phone", 699.99, 15)
    print("Available products:", list(shop.products.keys()))

    # Demonstrate adding to cart
    print("\n=== Cart Operations ===")
    shop.add_to_cart("user1", "Laptop", 2)
    shop.add_to_cart("user1", "Phone", 1)
    print("User1's cart:", shop.users["user1"]["cart"])

    # Demonstrate checkout
    print("\n=== Checkout ===")
    order_id = shop.checkout("user1")
    print(f"Order created with ID: {order_id}")
    print("User1's cart after checkout:", shop.users["user1"]["cart"])

    # Demonstrate order status update
    print("\n=== Order Status Update ===")
    shop.update_order_status(order_id, "processing")
    print(f"Order {order_id} status:", shop.orders[order_id]["status"])

    # Demonstrate system state
    print("\n=== System State ===")
    state = shop.get_system_state()
    print("Users:", state["users"])
    print("Products:", state["products"])
    print("Orders:", state["orders"])

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
