===== USE_CASE_EXTRACTION_FAILED OUTPUT =====
Here is the structured Use Case derived from the provided program:

---

**Use Case: Manage E-Shop Operations**

**Primary Actor:** User (Customer, Administrator)

**Goal:**
- Manage user accounts, products, shopping carts, and orders in an e-shop system.
- Ensure proper stock management, order processing, and system state tracking.

---

### **System Responsibilities:**
1. **User Management**
   - Create, delete, and track user accounts.
   - Maintain user-specific shopping carts and product reservations.

2. **Product Management**
   - Add, update, and delete products with pricing and stock information.
   - Track physical stock and reserved quantities across users.

3. **Shopping Cart Management**
   - Allow users to add/remove products to/from their carts.
   - Reserve products in cart to prevent overselling.

4. **Order Processing**
   - Process user checkouts to create orders.
   - Update order statuses with valid transitions (e.g., pending → processing → completed).
   - Handle order cancellations and restocking.

5. **System State Monitoring**
   - Provide a read-only snapshot of the current system state (users, products, orders, reservations).

---

### **Preconditions:**
1. The e-shop system is initialized and operational.
2. For user-specific actions (e.g., add to cart), the user must exist in the system.
3. For product-specific actions (e.g., add to cart), the product must exist in the system.
4. For order status updates, the order must exist in the system.

---

### **Postconditions:**
1. **User Management**
   - New users are added to the system with empty carts and reservations.
   - Deleted users have their carts cleared, reservations released, and accounts removed.

2. **Product Management**
   - New products are added with specified price and stock.
   - Updated products reflect new price/stock values.
   - Deleted products are removed from the system.

3. **Shopping Cart Management**
   - Products added to a cart are reserved for the user.
   - Products removed from a cart have their reservations released.

4. **Order Processing**
   - Successful checkouts create orders with a unique ID, pending status, and stock deducted.
   - Order status updates follow valid transitions (e.g., pending → processing).
   - Cancelled orders restock the associated products.

5. **System State Monitoring**
   - The system state snapshot reflects the latest data (users, products, orders, reservations).

---

### **Main Success Scenario:**

#### **1. Create User Account**
1. Actor requests to create a new user account with a unique ID.
2. System validates the ID (non-empty and not already in use).
3. System creates the user account with an empty cart and reservations.
4. System confirms success.

#### **2. Add Product to Inventory**
1. Actor requests to add a product with a name, price, and stock quantity.
2. System validates the input (non-empty name, non-negative price/stock).
3. System adds the product or updates stock/price if it already exists.
4. System confirms success.

#### **3. Add Product to Cart**
1. Actor requests to add a product to their cart with a specified quantity.
2. System validates:
   - User exists.
   - Product exists.
   - Quantity is positive and does not exceed available stock (physical stock minus existing reservations).
3. System updates the user’s cart and reserves the product quantity.
4. System confirms success.

#### **4. Remove Product from Cart**
1. Actor requests to remove a product from their cart with a specified quantity.
2. System validates:
   - User exists.
   - Product is in the user’s cart.
   - Quantity does not exceed the cart quantity.
3. System updates the cart and releases the reserved quantity.
4. System confirms success.

#### **5. Checkout**
1. Actor requests to checkout their cart.
2. System validates:
   - User exists and has items in their cart.
   - All products in the cart are available (stock ≥ cart quantity after other reservations).
3. System:
   - Calculates the total price.
   - Creates an order with a unique ID, pending status, and price snapshot.
   - Deducts stock for all products in the order.
   - Clears the user’s cart and reservations.
4. System returns the order ID.

#### **6. Update Order Status**
1. Actor requests to update an order’s status (e.g., pending → processing).
2. System validates:
   - Order exists.
   - New status is a valid transition from the current status.
3. System updates the order status.
4. If the new status is "cancelled," system restocks the order’s products.
5. System confirms success.

#### **7. Delete User Account**
1. Actor requests to delete a user account.
2. System validates the user exists.
3. System:
   - Releases all product reservations for the user.
   - Clears the user’s cart.
   - Deletes the user account.
4. System confirms success.

#### **8. Delete Product**
1. Actor requests to delete a product.
2. System validates the product exists.
3. System removes the product from the inventory.
4. System confirms success.

#### **9. Retrieve System State**
1. Actor requests the current system state.
2. System returns a read-only snapshot of:
   - Users (including carts and reservations).
   - Products (including stock).
   - Orders (including statuses).
   - Next available order ID.

---

### **Extensions:**

#### **1a. Create User Account Fails**
- **1a1.** User ID is empty or already exists.
  - System rejects the request and notifies the actor.

#### **2a. Add Product Fails**
- **2a1.** Product name is empty, or price/stock is negative.
  - System rejects the request and notifies the actor.

#### **3a. Add to Cart Fails**
- **3a1.** User does not exist.
  - System rejects the request and notifies the actor.
- **3a2.** Product does not exist.
  - System rejects the request and notifies the actor.
- **3a3.** Quantity is invalid (≤ 0 or exceeds available stock).
  - System rejects the request and notifies the actor.

#### **4a. Remove from Cart Fails**
- **4a1.** User does not exist.
  - System rejects the request and notifies the actor.
- **4a2.** Product is not in the user’s cart.
  - System rejects the request and notifies the actor.
- **4a3.** Quantity exceeds cart quantity.
  - System rejects the request and notifies the actor.

#### **5a. Checkout Fails**
- **5a1.** User does not exist or cart is empty.
  - System rejects the request and notifies the actor.
- **5a2.** Product in cart is unavailable (insufficient stock after reservations).
  - System rejects the request and notifies the actor.

#### **6a. Update Order Status Fails**
- **6a1.** Order does not exist.
  - System rejects the request and notifies the actor.
- **6a2.** New status is not a valid transition from the current status.
  - System rejects the request and notifies the actor.

#### **7a. Delete User Fails**
- **7a1.** User does not exist.
  - System rejects the request and notifies the actor.

#### **8a. Delete Product Fails**
- **8a1.** Product does not exist.
  - System rejects the request and notifies the actor.

---

### **Special Requirements:**
1. **Stock Management:**
   - The system must prevent overselling by tracking physical stock and reservations.
   - Reservations must be released when products are removed from carts or users are deleted.

2. **Order Status Transitions:**
   - The system must enforce valid status transitions (e.g., "pending" → "processing" but not "pending" → "delivered").

3. **Data Integrity:**
   - The system must ensure that order cancellations restock products accurately.
   - The system state snapshot must be read-only to prevent external modifications.

4. **Performance:**
   - Stock validation during cart operations must account for all existing reservations efficiently.

---

===== assemble_program OUTPUT =====
from copy import deepcopy
from types import MappingProxyType

class EShopSystem:
    def __init__(self):
        self.users = {}
        self.products = {}
        self.orders = {}
        self.next_order_id = 1
        self.reservations = {}

    def create_user(self, user_id):
        if not user_id or user_id in self.users:
            return False

        self.users[user_id] = {
            'cart': {},
            'reservations': {}
        }
        return True

    def add_product(self, product_name, price, stock):
        if not product_name or price < 0 or stock < 0:
            return False

        if product_name in self.products:
            self.products[product_name]['stock'] += stock
            self.products[product_name]['price'] = price
        else:
            self.products[product_name] = {
                'price': price,
                'stock': stock
            }

        return True

    def delete_product(self, product_name):
        if product_name not in self.products:
            return False

        del self.products[product_name]
        return True

    def get_system_state(self):
        """Retrieves the current system state, including users, products, physical stock, carts, and orders.

        Returns:
            dict: A read-only snapshot of the current system state, including:
                - users: Dictionary of user data including carts
                - products: Dictionary of product data including physical stock (reservations are not subtracted)
                - reservations: Dictionary of user reservations
                - orders: Dictionary of all orders
                - next_order_id: The next available order ID
        """
        state = {
            'users': deepcopy(self.users),
            'products': deepcopy(self.products),
            'orders': deepcopy(self.orders),
            'next_order_id': self.next_order_id,
            'reservations': deepcopy(self.reservations)
        }
        return MappingProxyType(state)

    def delete_user(self, user_id):
        if user_id not in self.users:
            return False

        # Process reservations
        user_reservations = self.users[user_id]['reservations'].copy()
        for product_name, quantity in user_reservations.items():
            if product_name in self.products:
                self.products[product_name]['stock'] += quantity
            del self.users[user_id]['reservations'][product_name]

        # Clear cart and reservations
        self.users[user_id]['cart'].clear()

        # Delete user
        del self.users[user_id]

        return True

    def add_to_cart(self, user_id, product_name, quantity):
        if quantity <= 0:
            return False

        if user_id not in self.users:
            return False

        if product_name not in self.products:
            return False

        physical_stock = self.products[product_name]['stock']
        existing_reservations = sum(
            self.users[uid]['reservations'].get(product_name, 0)
            for uid in self.users
            if product_name in self.users[uid]['reservations']
        )

        if quantity > (physical_stock - existing_reservations):
            return False

        if product_name in self.users[user_id]['cart']:
            self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] += quantity
        else:
            self.users[user_id]['cart'][product_name] = quantity

        if product_name in self.users[user_id]['reservations']:
            self.users[user_id]['reservations'][product_name] += quantity
        else:
            self.users[user_id]['reservations'][product_name] = quantity

        return True

    def remove_from_cart(self, user_id, product_name, quantity):
        if user_id not in self.users:
            return False

        user_cart = self.users[user_id]['cart']
        if product_name not in user_cart:
            return False

        reserved_quantity = user_cart[product_name]
        if quantity > reserved_quantity:
            return False

        if quantity == reserved_quantity:
            del user_cart[product_name]
        else:
            user_cart[product_name] -= quantity

        user_reservations = self.users[user_id]['reservations']
        if product_name in user_reservations:
            if quantity == user_reservations[product_name]:
                del user_reservations[product_name]
            else:
                user_reservations[product_name] -= quantity

        return True

    def checkout(self, user_id):
        if user_id not in self.users or not self.users[user_id]['cart']:
            return None

        cart = self.users[user_id]['cart']
        total_price = 0.0

        for product_name, quantity in cart.items():
            if product_name not in self.products:
                return None

            product = self.products[product_name]
            other_reservations = sum(
                self.users.get(other_user, {}).get('reservations', {}).get(product_name, 0)
                for other_user in self.users
                if other_user != user_id
            )
            available_stock = product['stock'] - other_reservations

            if available_stock < quantity:
                return None

            total_price += product['price'] * quantity

        order_id = f"SK{self.next_order_id:05d}"
        self.next_order_id += 1

        order = {
            'status': 'pending',
            'items': cart.copy(),
            'price_snapshot': {product: self.products[product]['price'] for product in cart},
            'total_price': total_price
        }
        self.orders[order_id] = order

        for product_name, quantity in cart.items():
            self.products[product_name]['stock'] -= quantity

        self.users[user_id]['cart'].clear()
        if user_id in self.users and 'reservations' in self.users[user_id]:
            self.users[user_id]['reservations'].clear()

        return order_id

    def update_order_status(self, order_id, new_status):
        if order_id not in self.orders:
            return False

        current_status = self.orders[order_id]['status']

        if current_status in ['cancelled', 'delivered']:
            if new_status != 'cancelled':
                return False

        valid_transitions = {
            'pending': ['processing', 'cancelled'],
            'processing': ['completed', 'cancelled'],
            'completed': ['shipped', 'cancelled'],
            'shipped': ['delivered', 'cancelled']
        }

        if new_status not in valid_transitions.get(current_status, []):
            return False

        if new_status == 'cancelled':
            for product_name, quantity in self.orders[order_id]['items'].items():
                if product_name in self.products:
                    self.products[product_name]['stock'] += quantity
                else:
                    price = self.orders[order_id]['price_snapshot'].get(product_name, 0.0)
                    self.products[product_name] = {
                        'price': price,
                        'stock': quantity
                    }

        self.orders[order_id]['status'] = new_status
        return True

def main():
    # Create an instance of the e-shop system
    shop = EShopSystem()

    # Demonstrate user management
    print("=== User Management ===")
    shop.create_user("user1")
    shop.create_user("user2")
    print("Created users:", list(shop.users.keys()))

    # Demonstrate product management
    print("\n=== Product Management ===")
    shop.add_product("Laptop", 999.99, 10)
    shop.add_product("Phone", 699.99, 15)
    print("Available products:", list(shop.products.keys()))

    # Demonstrate adding to cart
    print("\n=== Cart Operations ===")
    shop.add_to_cart("user1", "Laptop", 2)
    shop.add_to_cart("user1", "Phone", 1)
    print("User1's cart:", shop.users["user1"]["cart"])

    # Demonstrate checkout
    print("\n=== Checkout ===")
    order_id = shop.checkout("user1")
    print(f"Order created with ID: {order_id}")
    print("User1's cart after checkout:", shop.users["user1"]["cart"])

    # Demonstrate order status update
    print("\n=== Order Status Update ===")
    shop.update_order_status(order_id, "processing")
    print(f"Order {order_id} status:", shop.orders[order_id]["status"])

    # Demonstrate system state
    print("\n=== System State ===")
    state = shop.get_system_state()
    print("Users:", state["users"])
    print("Products:", state["products"])
    print("Orders:", state["orders"])

    # Demonstrate edge cases
    print("\n=== Edge Cases ===")
    # Checkout failure due to insufficient stock
    shop.add_product("Tablet", 399.99, 1)
    shop.add_to_cart("user2", "Tablet", 1)
    shop.add_to_cart("user1", "Tablet", 1)  # Should fail
    print("User1's cart after failed addition:", shop.users["user1"]["cart"])

    # Order cancellation
    order_id2 = shop.checkout("user2")
    print(f"Order created with ID: {order_id2}")
    shop.update_order_status(order_id2, "cancelled")
    print(f"Order {order_id2} status after cancellation:", shop.orders[order_id2]["status"])

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

===== generate_integration_test OUTPUT =====
def test_main_success_scenario():
    system = EShopSystem()

    # Create a user
    assert system.create_user("user1") is True

    # Add a product
    assert system.add_product("laptop", 999.99, 10) is True

    # Add product to cart
    assert system.add_to_cart("user1", "laptop", 2) is True

    # Perform checkout
    order_id = system.checkout("user1")
    assert order_id is not None

    # Verify order exists
    state = system.get_system_state()
    assert order_id in state["orders"]
    assert state["orders"][order_id]["status"] == "pending"

def test_user_deletion_with_reservations():
    system = EShopSystem()

    # Create user and add product
    assert system.create_user("user2") is True
    assert system.add_product("phone", 599.99, 5) is True

    # Add product to cart and checkout
    assert system.add_to_cart("user2", "phone", 3) is True
    order_id = system.checkout("user2")
    assert order_id is not None

    # Delete user
    assert system.delete_user("user2") is True

    # Verify user and order are gone
    state = system.get_system_state()
    assert "user2" not in state["users"]
    assert order_id in state["orders"]  # Order should remain

def test_product_deletion_with_active_orders():
    system = EShopSystem()

    # Create user and add product
    assert system.create_user("user3") is True
    assert system.add_product("tablet", 399.99, 8) is True

    # Add product to cart and checkout
    assert system.add_to_cart("user3", "tablet", 2) is True
    order_id = system.checkout("user3")
    assert order_id is not None

    # Delete product
    assert system.delete_product("tablet") is True

    # Verify product is gone but order remains
    state = system.get_system_state()
    assert "tablet" not in state["products"]
    assert order_id in state["orders"]

===== OUTPUT_generate_activity_diagram OUTPUT =====
@startuml
start
:initialize e-shop system;
:create user1 and user2;
:add Laptop and Phone products;
:add 2 Laptops and 1 Phone to user1's cart;
if (cart not empty?) then (yes)
    :generate order ID;
    :validate product availability;
    if (all products available?) then (yes)
        :calculate total price;
        :create order with pending status;
        :reduce stock for all products;
        :clear user's cart and reservations;
        :return order ID;
    else (no)
        :cancel checkout;
        :return None;
    endif
else (no)
    :return None;
endif
:update order status to processing;
if (order exists?) then (yes)
    :check current status;
    if (valid transition?) then (yes)
        if (new status is cancelled?) then (yes)
            :restore stock for all products;
            if (product exists?) then (yes)
                :increase stock;
            else (no)
                :recreate product with original price;
            endif
        endif
        :update order status;
    else (no)
        :ignore request;
    endif
else (no)
    :ignore request;
endif
:get system state;
:return read-only snapshot;
:demonstrate edge cases;
if (insufficient stock?) then (yes)
    :fail to add to cart;
else (no)
    :add to cart;
endif
if (checkout successful?) then (yes)
    :create order;
    :cancel order;
    :restore stock;
endif
stop
@enduml

===== OUTPUT_review_activity_diagram OUTPUT =====
Here are the identified defects in the activity diagram:

- Missing `@enduml` tag at the end of the PlantUML code (syntax incomplete)
- The diagram shows `:return order ID;` after `:clear user's cart and reservations;` in the successful checkout flow, but the program actually returns the order ID immediately after creating the order (execution flow incorrect)
- The diagram includes `:update order status to processing;` as a linear step after checkout, but in the program this is a separate operation triggered by `update_order_status()` (execution flow incorrect)
- The diagram shows `:demonstrate edge cases;` as a distinct step, but this is not part of the actual program execution flow (behavior not present in program)
- The diagram does not show the atomic rollback behavior when checkout fails (e.g., no stock reduction if validation fails) (behavioral completeness missing)
- The diagram does not reflect the sequential order ID generation logic (`next_order_id`) shown in the program (behavioral completeness missing)
- The diagram shows `:restore stock for all products;` in the cancellation flow but does not distinguish between existing and non-existing products (as the program does) (abstraction level incorrect)
